# Tasks

One list again (2026-10-15): Tasks.md (1-199, 213-217) and Tasks2.md (200-229) merged. At the top a short status;
under "All tasks" every task as written; the notes ("For NNN") at the end.

## At a glance (2026-10-25)

- Tasks: 344 (numbered 1-344). Done: 156. Open: 188, most of them code done and waiting for a check in the headset.
- 344 (2026-10-27, work-until-limit 40, not pushed: the user's GitHub has problems): bug hunt, improve and optimize with two
  Haiku workflow rounds; the hand-to-body delay (the anchor at the step's own time, held bones drawn extrapolated), scout fixes
  (For 344). Tested only at the end, as the user asked.
- 1.13.0 (2026-10-25): 342 (the hand's grab anchor: no more flinging, spinning or long bodies; user: "fixed!!") and 343 (punches, neck, grenade countdown,
  gun and fist collisions with soldiers, the full magazine drawn full).
- 342: held soldiers spun, flung and slipped out of the hand walking or running because the grab anchor was kinematic (PhysX solved
  its joint as if it stood still); it is a dynamic body driven by velocity now, and snap turns no longer throw the body. Open: the
  hand-to-body delay.
- 1.12.3 published 2026-10-25 (https://github.com/QWavey/ravenfield-vr/releases/tag/v1.12.3): 341, held soldiers no longer
  fling (momentum matched at the grab, the hold's turn damped and torque-limited, snap turns carry the body). Waiting for the
  user's play: the soldier check's "running" case never pulled a body "long" as the user saw.
- 1.12.2 published 2026-10-25 (https://github.com/QWavey/ravenfield-vr/releases/tag/v1.12.2): 339 and 340 (the support
  hand slid onto the charging handle, the menu's code and scope warm-ups, the staged slide measurement, 339's batch).
  NEXT: 340's open items: FitMagazineLine's one-off frame (29.8 ms at most on a loaded PC, once a weapon type, PATRIOT GL
  in autotest-1025h; its volume tests could go to a worker), GREASER's rack grip (its bolt is inside the receiver tube: a
  grip design, not a fix), the neck check's soldier facing about 90 degrees off on a slow PC.
- 1.12.0 published 2026-10-24 night (https://github.com/QWavey/ravenfield-vr/releases/tag/v1.12.0), from bb31d1b: the
  soldiers (336-338), the retuned presets, the wrist's frame rate and GPU time.
- 1.12.1 published 2026-10-25 (https://github.com/QWavey/ravenfield-vr/releases/tag/v1.12.1, f024b6f): Setup's icon in
  Chrome's downloads. Every icon frame was PNG; Chrome reads a downloaded exe's icon in a sandbox that cannot decode PNG
  frames and showed the generic program icon. tools/make_icon.py writes bitmaps below 256 and PNG at 256 now (as Rufus,
  Claude's and EA's installers), plus 20/24/40 px. User to confirm with a fresh Chrome download of 1.12.1.
- 2026-10-24 night (work-until-limit 75, game runs allowed): 338 = the soldier features cleaned up and made cheaper (review
  workflow of 6 Haiku readers, every finding checked by a skeptic), the knife no longer hits on the trigger, a frozen
  two-round graphics bench, Ultra's forced texture filter dropped (8.9 -> 6.0 ms GPU). Estimated frame rates: For 338.
- 2026-10-24 afternoon (no game run): 336 = the presets' ghosting (retuned for multi-pass; the player's own body shadow hidden,
  the likelier cause), soldiers grabbed and thrown with Boneworks' speed-squared impact damage, fists and whole-gun bashes,
  a once-a-minute frame-rate log line to measure the presets on the user's PC. Check in the headset: For 336's list in its entry.
- 2026-10-23/24 (no game run): 334 = Boneworks studied and its hand and reload behaviour imported (glove contact sweep,
  hands stay on worked parts, slam release, haptic stack; the user's hand-through-gun bug), released as 1.11.0; 335 =
  measured improve rounds and read-only Haiku hunts (scouts and three workflows), every finding with its status in
  reports/haiku-findings-2026-10-23.txt and -24.txt. Check in the headset first: For 334's list, then a -rfvr-selftest
  run for the two measured-first analysis notes (For 335).
- 1.11.1 published 2026-10-24 (https://github.com/QWavey/ravenfield-vr/releases/tag/v1.11.1): this run's fixes, speed-ups and
  setup hardening.

216. [ ] (2026-10-15) The wrist view still flickers very fast (everything appears and disappears) when looking at the left wrist, after the 2 s hold. Suspected: a loop in the input bridge: the scoreboard/map counts as an open menu (IsCursorFree via ScoreboardUi.IsOpenAndFocused), the bridge returns early on a menu and stops holding the Map/Scoreboard keys, the game closes them, next frame they are held again. Fix: hold the wrist view's keys before the menu early return (code done 2026-10-15: confirmed in the game's code: the Scoreboard key opens the scoreboard focused (ScoreboardUi.isFocused), which frees the cursor (FpsActorController.IsCursorFree), which the bridge took for an open menu and let the key go: closed, opened, every other frame. The wrist view now holds PeekScoreboard (as the game's own SteamVR binding does), never focused. New autotest check: held 3.6 s, it opens at 2 s and the scoreboard and map never toggle, no menu counted open, match bar shown, all closed after)

(b) RPGs still shake a lot while held: steadier hold, and MORE recoil when fired (not less); (c) mwii-g17 family: the slide goes forward on its own on an empty-magazine reload (it should stay locked until the slide release or a rack); (d) FNX-45 (all variants): after an empty reload the racked slide stays back until the first shot; the magazine shows inside the handle again (check every pistol for both, some were fixed before); (e) M1 GARRET (vanilla Garand, see 218): does not lock open on the last round, a new clip cannot be put in, no ping and the clip does not fly out (use the game's own ping sound, do not make one), and the pouch hands out an EMPTY clip; (f) M4A1 (the not-[SQUAD] ones): the charging handle stays back on an empty magazine (on an M4 the bolt locks, the handle goes home); (g) hands and magazines pass through weapons when moved fast enough (no tunnelling: sweep between frames); (h) on MANY pistols the magazine goes in beside the well / shows inside the handle while inserted (the autotest flags it: "GOES IN BESIDE THE WELL", e.g. 51-67% on the mwii-g17 family); (i) on MANY pistols the slide goes forward on its own once a magazine is in, or stays back even when pulled and let go (until the first shot); (j) some pistols show a second magazine (the reload animation's spare) behind the pistol model: hide it; (k) MK18RII: its flashlight and laser shine backwards, behind the player (seen only with the gun turned round); (l) flashlights and lasers switched by hand: the off hand on the gun (handguard / finger rest) presses its own trigger to cycle the modes the gun offers (laser, light, both, off); (m) on primary weapons (some or all) grabbing the seated magazine, even gently, pulls it out with no slide, a little sideways, and it jumps to the hand: it must come out the way it went in (along the well); (n) machine guns: on some the box / belt in the hand is invisible (only the round count shows), on some only the belt comes out and not the box itself; (o) after all of the above: improve the machine gun reload as a whole; (p) two weapons at once (with 152): taking a second weapon makes the first one disappear. Allowed pairs, one per hand: primary + secondary, primary + launcher, primary + grenade, secondary + grenade, secondary + launcher, grenade + launcher; never primary + primary; (q) under-barrel grenade launchers (HK416-D): the support hand on the launcher fires it with its own trigger (with (l): on the launcher the off hand's trigger fires it, elsewhere on the gun it cycles the lights and lasers); (r) no knock sounds when a magazine meets the gun (too goofy); (h, more) the HK416-D and the CZ Bren 2 BR show the magazine going in through the gun too: likely every kind of weapon; (s) the QJB machine guns' feed covers cannot be opened; (t) shotgun pumps: some cannot be racked, others rack all the time because the support hand holds the pump: the pump is only worked while the off hand's trigger is held on it, otherwise it is just the support grip; (u) check every shotgun can be reloaded; (v) double-barrel shotguns break open but the spent shells do not come out, and the shells are not the gun's own shell models; (w) night vision is only grey, and drawn on the flat screen, not in the headset all round; (x) PPSh-41 (both): the support hand holds it in the air off the gun, and the grip stretches away to infinity; (y) a two-magazine (palmed) reload: the magazine taken out and held with the fresh one passes through the gun; both must stay out of it; (z) walking or running, the hands glitch and reloading is almost impossible; (aa) MODDED grenades are held just off the hand, in the air beside the glove, not in it (user: the modded ones; the vanilla FRAG looked right in the test picture). The fix in 49b54e8 (held by the drawn middle) only applies when the drawn bounds are under 20 cm and it is not a kit: check the modded grenades' log line 'held by its middle', and widen it if their bounds take in helper meshes; (ad) no button switches full auto / single (user's headset test, 23:30 build): where the gun supports it (the game's sub weapons / SwitchFireMode, or a script's fire-mode key, e.g. X in flat play, different per mod) a button must switch it, and nothing where it does not; check the FireMode binding (hold A on the gun hand), GameInputBridge 245-264 and WeaponSystem 480 (ScriptKeys FireMode) per gun; (ae) some charging handles still cannot be worked (machine guns' and others'): verify on every gun that the hand pulls back the gun's own handle or bolt and not something else, with rack pictures of every gun (next session; 139, 245, 246); (af) machine guns are very buggy: firing and opening the cover work, but reloading by hand is very buggy and does not work at all (the automatic reload does): with (n) and (o); (ab) hitting enemies with a weapon (a swing, not a shot) does no damage; (ac) mwii-m4holo: a black cube by the charging handle (the added knob, 245) and the handle itself does not move; (i, more) primaries too: the charging handle pulled back stays back until the first shot (the same as the pistols' slides; LeaveEmptyPose in 8bf8e78 covers rifles as well: to check in the headset)

[ ] PRIORITY REGRESSION (cause found and fixed 2026-10-16 23:50, to check in the headset: in HeldWeapon.ApplyPartOverrides the slide's 'home once' else-block was left hanging off the new 'if (boltAlone) ... else if (_boltMoved)' (8bf8e78), so it ran every frame and sent the slide / handle home in the same frame it was moved: no slide, bolt or handle could be pulled back by hand; the gun's own fire animation still moved them). The handles that still moved in that build were the mod's added knobs / cut pieces (their offset is set after the broken block), and the bolt behind them did not move because the block reset it: the same cause. The added knob now sits on the surface (1 mm, was 4 mm). Also reported the same night: assault rifles the same; RECOIL mostly gone on most weapons; RPGs held a little wrongly. Not explained by the else-block (no shot drives SlideOffset). Leads for recoil: compare a shot's PeakKick / PeakBack in the autotest's recoil lines with the 21:15 run (one hand ~8 deg, two hands ~4 deg on pistols); the analysis change for long guns whose animation's left hand was behind the grip (they are no longer 'pistols': Handling, Supported, the two-hand steer); the launcher hold steadied as through a 3x sight and its damped spring (01556f6); for the RPGs also the new forend support point turning a two-handed launcher toward the support hand.  (user, 2026-10-16 23:40, headset, build c861225a / a77a980): almost all pistols' slides can no longer be pulled back by hand (the slide still moves when fired, but a hand on the slide pulling moves nothing). Caused by tonight's 252 changes (the 22:30 build could pull them). Also: some charging handles can now be pulled but stand out of the gun a little too far, and the bolt inside does not move with them. Plan: first thing next session: rack check on a handful of pistols (RackGripSelfCheck: "drawn: N part(s) move"), the log for exceptions while racking, then bisect the 252 commits (8bf8e78 slides/handles/magwell, 01556f6, 2a81eaa line fit, 1035a95 pump, 73965c8 support trigger, 75638b2 bash, 38965e9 T handle) if not obvious. Suspects to check first: the grab choice between the slide (rack) and the pistol cup now that the cup runs SupportTrigger every frame; BoltLockedAlone / the BoltPath of the "charging handle carrying the bolt" rule if it caught pistols (the slide part becoming a parent and the real slide its "bolt"); an exception in HeldWeapon.Update / BeforeRender (LeaveEmptyPose, TurnEmittersForward, FitMagazineLine, Bash) stopping ApplyPartOverrides; LeaveEmptyPose cross-fading the animator into a state that drives the slide every frame. The handles standing out: the added T latch (BoltPiece, 4 mm off the surface) and the carried bolt not moving (BoltPath only moves when locked).

[ ] New workshop mods (user, 2026-10-18 evening: "also for new workshop mods the mags and reloadable parts etc should be found dynamically, lets not forget that; maybe it does that already when it loads the mods"). It does: every installed weapon is analysed when the game has loaded its content (menu warm-up, else on first use), the magazine and the rack from the weapon's own reload and fire animation, names only as a tie-break or fallback, no per-weapon list (287 exported rigs: 81% of magazines and 74% of racks from motion). Gaps found by 5 read-only Haiku scouts, to fix: stale rigs after the menu's mod reload (caches never cleared), one rule throwing loses the whole rig for the session (and a throw outside the try reruns every frame), two mods' weapons with the same entry + prefab names share one rig / drop shape / hull / pose, silent fallbacks (virtual magazine or rack on shell-fed and single-shot guns warned nowhere), hand bones by English names only, a flat hierarchy's unnamed meshes never parts, the sandbox not seeing new mods until an export. See "For 262". Fixed by the bug hunt of 2026-10-19 (282): the name-keyed caches are dropped on a content reload (the analysis, hulls, fits, sound donors, drop shapes and templates, magazine looks, slide shares), each analysis rule is guarded on its own (a throwing rule leaves its own result out) and a weapon whose analysis setup threw is analysed again in the next scene, drop shapes are keyed per weapon entry. Still open: the analysis key per entry for two same-named mods, the silent fallbacks, non-English hand bones, flat hierarchies, the sandbox and new mods

BoltPiece then adds no knob (the carrier moves with the hand), so no second handle comes back. To check in the headset and with the autotest's rack check.
270. [ ] Pistols: the magazine slides in through the grip instead of up its well (user, 2026-10-18 evening, headset; also seen in the sandbox on the M1911 during the reload). Changed in code, to check (2026-10-18): the magazine's line is its own length (13-21 deg off its box on the 1911); for a pistol the line is now fitted against the magazine's drawn shape in the grip, not its box. The sandbox draws the analysed line, not the fitted one. Third look (2026-10-18 evening, after the user's "still there"): a Haiku fixer measured the 1911's export: the in-game fit sampled a grid inside the magazine (never its skin) and judged the well by the magazine's own hollow depth span, so it saw 0 through; by the skin, 163 points pass through the grip over the way in on the analysed line. Changed in code: a handgun's line is now fitted by its magazine's skin against its seated hull over the whole way in. But a full tilt scan of the 1911 (-18..+18 deg) shows the analysed line is already the best (every tilt is worse: 260-1520 points), so the line is not the cause on the 1911: the rest is the seat (it overlaps the back strap 3-5 mm at its base pad) or the magazine in the hand before the rail (MagazineProp.KeepOffGun / ProbeInside still use the magazine's box + 8 mm as the open well, so a held magazine can pass the grip's walls). Next: those two, with a picture of the 1911 in the headset. CAUSE FOUND (2026-10-19; the user: "this happens to some m4 versions too, check the simulator and trace the animations"): the sandbox's reload and a skin scan of the export show the ANALYSED lines are clean (the M4A1 [SQUAD] and reddot: 0 skin points through at any depth; 6 deg off: some), but the GAME's fit (HeldWeapon.FitMagazineLine) tilted lines on 3-4 stray grid points of some 300 ("a small tilt that clears it completely"): the M4A1 (m4) -6 deg, the M4A1 EOT and EOT FT -3, the SpringField 1911 +3, and tilted, the magazine went in through the gun. Fixed: every gun is fitted by its magazine's skin against its seated hull, and tilted only on a clear fault (1% of the points at least, 40% fewer after). check-1019c: all four kept (M4A1 3/1494, EOT 1/1494, SpringField 0, [SQUAD] 0). To check in the headset. See "For 270" FOUND AND FIXED (2026-10-19 evening; user: "did you fix the handle bug with the reloads? the 1911s"): (1) a magazine off its line was taken onto its rail anywhere up the well (4.5 cm aside, 40 deg) and slid in through the grip's wall in the 0.08 s switch (with the old rule the new autotest phase had two 1911s taken beside the grip, 276-278 points through the walls); now taken loosely only at the mouth (judged where the magazine is drawn, so a fast push held back at the mouth still counts), deeper only on its line (6 mm, 12 deg), and put on at the mouth itself. (2) The rail of a pistol magazine began 6.0-6.7 cm up inside the 1911s' grips (12.6-13.2 cm deep from the side; the Glock 43X 5.2 of 10.8), where the magazine was still free and could come in turned 40 deg: the rail of a well in the grip now starts at the grip's bottom (measured from the side once per hold), and past the mouth the well is open only to a magazine within 12 deg. New autotest phase in the deep magazine check (wells in the grip): from the side at half depth, and turned 25 deg from below. check-1019k: turned 25 deg 0 points through the walls on all four pistols (was 15-45), from the side kept out at the wall (never taken), every reload seats. Still open: on the rail at mid depth 38-58% of the part gone in shows beside the grip's sides against 0-29% seated (a thin sliver of the magazine's top edge through the 1911's grip panel in the pictures), and a magazine pushed sideways into the grip can sink into its wall (the keep-out tests 45 points). To check in the headset. Offline check of the sliver (2026-10-19 night, export + CPU raster, magline_scan): on the Springfield 1911s the analysed line puts 0 magazine points through the grip's sides at any depth and keeps the magazine inside the grip's side outline (the 1-3% "beside" points are at the mouth's edge, a measuring boundary); its own long axis is 0.3-1.6 deg off the line. So the game's "goes in beside the well" (38-58%) and the sliver are not the line: next, in the game, compare the prop's pose and shape with the export and check InsideGunFromSide, with a close-up from each side. The autotest's "goes in beside the well" was itself wrong (2026-10-19 night): it compared the half-way magazine's points with the same points of the seated magazine, which sit up in the frame, instead of with the seated magazine at the same place in the gun; the grip's bottom (a cutout, a flared well) shows more of any magazine there, so a clean line was flagged on the 1911s and the Glock. Fixed (the point slid down the line by the part still out): on a clean line the two now agree, and the flag means a magazine leaving its own well. Re-check the 1911s with it.

278. [ ] The lens flash (the square effect on the lenses) does not come on some guns although their muzzle flash shows (user, 2026-10-19). Cause: LensFlash shows only when the gun's own flash particles are seen to come out on the shot; on the mod-pack guns ([SQUAD] M4s, MWII M4s, XM8s, F2000s, AK-12s, PPSh drum, AS Vals) they never are ("its flash particles did not come out on the shot" on every shot, weapons-1017 too). Fixed in code (2026-10-19): a gun whose flash plays on every shot (its chance 1) flashes the lens on every shot; the particle count only for chance flashes (a first try, every shot after 3 unseen ones, never fired: a burst counted as seen). check-1019e: the PPSh drum's single shot shown (1.19), the M4A1 reddot's burst 18 of 18 (18 of 20 before); its single shot aimed through the red dot stays hidden by the sight, as meant. To check in the headset
279. [ ] Tooling: every vrshots VR run's first launch fails and costs 25-30 s ("came up without the headset; trying again"). Cause (2026-10-19, the failed launch's log now kept as reports/shots/failed-launch-N.log): SteamVR's room setup on the fresh null driver asks the game to quit, the autotest ignores it ("SteamVR asked the game to quit; ignored during the autotest"), and SteamVR ends the game about 23 s later; the second launch works. Starting SteamVR before the game did not help (the first launch then failed after 90 s; reverted). This OpenVR has no AcknowledgeQuit_UserPrompt to hold the timeout. Next: give the null driver a chaperone (universe) so room setup never opens Tried (2026-10-19): the game logs the tracking universe (the null driver's is 2), and vrshots gives universe 2 a 3 x 3 m play area for the run (Steam\config\chaperone_info.vrchap; the user has none, so it is removed after the run, or backed up and put back by hash). FIXED (2026-10-19): with the play area every first launch came up in VR (check-1019i, j, k: no room setup, no lost 25 s); the chaperone file was removed again after each run.
280. [x] A "Ravenfield VR" entry in the list Steam shows when you press Play on Ravenfield (next to "Ravenfield (No Mods)" and "Ravenfield (Safe Mode)"), added by Setup and removed on uninstall (user, 2026-10-19). Those entries are Valve's app info, cached by Steam in appcache\appinfo.vdf (binary KeyValues v29 with a string table; app 636480 > appinfo > config > launch); Steam checks each record's SHA-1 over its key-value bytes (matches all 954 cached apps), while the record's text SHA-1 is Valve's and does not match a local rebuild for 855 of 954 untouched apps, so it is left as sent. installer/SteamPlayEntry.cs adds entry 17 (ravenfield.exe -vr, "Ravenfield VR", type option3, oslist windows) before the launch block's end, redoes the record size and binary SHA-1, reads the new file back whole before replacing Steam's (backup appinfo.vdf.rfvr-backup), only with Steam closed (Setup closes and restarts it with permission, as for the launch option); uninstall removes it. The launcher starts VR at once when the command has -vr (the entry) or -novr. Setup flags: --steam-play-entry add|remove, --steam-play-test FILE, --no-steam-play-entry. Tested 2026-10-19: add + remove on a copy gives back the file byte for byte, an independent parser reads the added entry and every other record unchanged; live: Steam closed, entry added, Steam started, the entry shows in Ravenfield's Play dialog (user) and is still in the cache after Steam's start-up. A Ravenfield update on Valve's side replaces the record and drops the entry; running Setup again adds it back.
281. [ ] No headset connected when VR is started (user, 2026-10-19: "Ravenfield VR" from Steam with the Quest 3 off gave "VR could not start: OpenVR failed to initialise: Init_HmdNotFound (108)", its last line cut off; "maybe add a try again button that initializes it again without restarting the game"). Done in code (2026-10-19): (1) the launcher asks SteamVR first, as a background app (never starts SteamVR, not shown in the headset; Valve's openvr_api.cs built into the launcher, the game's openvr_api.dll): headset there -> the game as before; none -> a "Waiting for the headset" window that starts SteamVR (again after 30 s if it went), says what to do (Steam Link, Air Link, Link cable, Virtual Desktop) and starts the game in VR by itself once SteamVR sees the headset, with Play flat / Start VR anyway / Cancel; an unknown answer (no openvr_api.dll, another error) lets the game try as before. (2) In the game the notice is 120 px high (the third line was cut off at 90), has Try again (F6) and Play flat buttons, and stays while the cursor is free (the menus; 40 s in a match, F6 after that). Tested 2026-10-19 on the null driver: ravenfield.exe -vr with no headset showed the notice; the null headset switched on, Try again clicked: "Trying VR again." and "VR active." in the same game (no restart); SteamVR settings restored. Seen there: SteamVR's own "critical error" window covers the notice's buttons while it is up (SteamVR's, with no headset). To check with the Quest: the waiting window, and Try again after connecting.
282. [ ] Bug hunt 2026-10-19 (user: "/bug-hunt 10 with haiku5.5 agents", and "did you check that the grips on weapons are all right and the hand cant go anywhere else except that grip (only if the user pulls hard away) and also scope stabilisation when aiming"): 10 rounds of Haiku hunters with a skeptic per finding, then fixers per file (own worktree, no build or game) and a verifier each; 55 found, 38 confirmed; merged 2026-10-19 by the lead (built after each merge). Fixed in code, to check in the headset: (grips) the support hand's stretch is measured on the gun as the gun hand alone holds it, so a hard sideways pull now lets go (steered, the live gun followed the palm and the stretch never grew), from a rest that follows the wrist's slow settling into a two-handed aim (lead's change: 7-17 cm of wrist offset would have buzzed at 15 cm) with the live handguard line still counted; the RPG-7 rear grip and magazine holds (PPSh drum) are measured from their own point and from where the hold took it (no buzz at rest); the support glove stays on its point when the snap is lost; a new rest per hold; a pistol with no left-hand bone settles its support glove on the cup. (steadying) the hand speed that opens the scope filter is a signed low-passed turn, so a shake no longer opens its own filter (the bigger the shake the less it was steadied); the ScopeSteadying setting is applied once (25% gave about 16%). (reloads, drops) a gun put down with its magazine out or locked back comes back so; launcher pickups go to slot 3; a stale active slot no longer draws the returned gun or swaps the drawn one; rounds of a swapped-away own gun's magazine come back; drop shapes per weapon entry, not per name. (racking) a hand-held open action keeps its handle. (input) gun buttons do nothing while the laser is on a mod panel (also the pin flick, the pump trigger, the seat buttons); a waiting fire-mode press is dropped on a weapon, menu or seat change; a shift tap during a sprint no longer presses Shift again for every script; the weapon hand takes effect on restart only (the bindings are mirrored at start); turret aim follows the gun hand. (second weapon) the gun hand cannot draw the second weapon from its hidden holster (it put both guns away). (analysis) each rule is guarded on its own (one throw replaced the whole rig with the fallback), failed analyses are tried again in the next scene, a content reload drops the name-keyed caches, overrides match whole names (contains: true for a part). (effects) the world muzzle glow is drawn at its own strength (0.02 was drawn at 0.1). (rig) no room-scale walking while paused, the use ray and the ladder check take the head's orientation, the body is kept out of the view under third-person or override cameras on foot (plausible, not seen in play). (MG3) lifting the cover takes a hand-laid belt off in the state too, and a laid belt shows after a redraw or spawn. Reverted by its verifier: the Touch bindings change for the squad hold (it removed the long-A fire mode that sheathes the katana and switches the helicopter's auto hover); see 286. Open follow-ups: 283-292.
283. [ ] Two-hand steering has no limit: the support hand pulled sideways swings the gun to any angle off the gun hand (bug hunt round 1). With 282 the hold lets go past 60 cm of pull from the gun-hand line, but the gun still bends with the pull until then. Proposed (reviewer): turn the bore at most about 20-30 deg off the gun hand (RotateTowards); changes every two-handed aim, so it needs the user's feel in the headset first.
284. [ ] Aim steadying for iron sights and red dots (zoom under 1.8x is not steadied at all; user, 2026-10-19: "scope stabilisation when aiming because the guns shake very much (when not shooting) because of my hands"; bug hunt round 1: a 1 cm off-hand tremor is about 2.4 deg at the barrel unfiltered). Done in code (2026-10-19): AimSteadying (Weapons, default 50%, "Aim steadying" in the VR settings panel) steadies the shake aimed down a sight with no magnification, with the scope filter (cut above about 1.7 Hz at 1x, a swing opens it to 8 Hz, recoil after it); ScopeSteadying stays for magnifiers. To check in the headset: is 50% enough, is a quick target switch still followed.

288. 293. [ ] Bug hunt and improve rounds, solo, no game (user, 2026-10-19 night: "do some improvement runs yourself and bug hunts but dont start ravenfield because im playing; dont launch haiku agents"). Bug hunt: (1) recent changes: a seated pistol magazine pulled out by hand or dropped by the release still had the short rail and came off half way up the grip (now the grip-bottom rail too); the launcher's headset check stayed a SteamVR background app if a step after connecting failed (now always shut); the support-pull autotest grabbed from 40 cm off the gun (now at the support point). (2) magazine keep-out: past the well's mouth the well was open to a magazine 4.5 cm off its line and a pistol magazine's outer 1.5-3 mm were never tested, so pushed in from the side it stood in the grip's wall (check-1019k: 23-102 probe points); now open only within 6 mm past the mouth, pistol magazines tested to their edges. (3) UI (settings panel, laser module, canvas manager): clean. (4) the installers (Setup.exe, install.ps1): an upgrade backed up a newly replaced original into a new stamped folder the install record never named, so uninstall left it modded (shown with an offline test of the old install.ps1); files a version dropped lingered; a failed upgrade lost the install record. All three fixed; offline test of the fixed scripts: both originals back, nothing left. (5) grenades and melee: clean (one suspected listener pile-up was not real: the handler is released on every weapon change). Improve: the lens flash no longer walks the muzzle tree and makes 6-10 arrays on every shot (cached per weapon for a second); SnapHands no longer builds two log strings and an extra hand pose every frame on guns whose hand bone is off the grip (RPG-7); build warnings 33 -> 0 (dead code in the gun bash, doc comments on the wrong members, missing param notes). Investigated, no change: the 1911 sliver (task 270: offline the line is clean). (6) magazine line vs the magazine's own shape, over the export (29 self-loaders with a long magazine): 108 of 287 guns have meshes the game keeps only on the graphics card, and for them the analysis cannot read the magazine's vertices and takes its bounding box, which stands upright: 87 self-loaders with a magazine get that box axis. The Vector family (4 rigs) is the clear case: its magazine is raked 18 degrees and the analysed line is straight down, so offline 52-78 of its points come out through the front of the well on the way in; along its own axis, none. The graphics-card reader cannot run in game (graphics jobs are on, so the device context is shared with the render thread), but the runtime fit (task 270, tilts the line up to 27 degrees until the drawn magazine skin stops going through the drawn gun) covers it: 18 degrees forward clears it offline. Curved magazines (AK-24, PPSh stick) are fine along their tops; the Quicksilver's magazine sits out the side of its gun (not a line fault). No code change. (7) export checks kept in tools/export_checks (magazine line scan and view, magazine axis audit, bolt-carrier rule, and a new hold-points audit). The hold-points audit found the three "Bot Friendly" ARS guns (MP9, HK416, Glock 19: no arms, muzzle set up a metre ahead and 5-7 cm to one side for a bot): their bore and estimated grip ran beside the gun and the hand held it 7-12 cm off its grip; a bore within 1 cm of no drawn part is now centred across the gun when that puts it on the gun (only those three over the export). The support points 6-15 cm to the side of the bore on most two-handed guns are the palms' sides, matched by the real palms in the two-hand steer: not a fault. (8) config: a vector setting written as NaN or Infinity hid the hand and gun (now refused like any malformed value). (9) a second weapon destroyed under the free hand (a pickup into its slot) left that hand closing on the destroyed weapon's shape. (10) holsters after turning: the torso yaw the holsters, pouch and stock pocket hang from was kept in the world and caught up with a turned head at 25 degrees a second, so for a second after each snap turn they hung 30 degrees off the body (a shoulder holster 9 cm from where the hand reached) and through a smooth turn they trailed 35 degrees; now kept relative to the play space, so turning carries them along (to check in the headset: snap turn, then draw at once; smooth turn while drawing). Clean: HolsterDisplay, the view effects' textures (VRScope, LensEffects, EyeFilm). Clean: AmmoState, WeaponDrops, Casings, VRRig, CockpitControls (two-hand code itself), GrenadeDud, PouchDisplay, the per-frame logging, raw LookRotation calls. To check when the game is free: the side-entry phase (keep-out, target 0 probe points), a full-auto run (no GC from the lens flash), the support-pull check on the RPG-7 and MG3, the Vector's log line "magazine line fitted to the drawn gun, turned about -18 deg".
294. [ ] Driving: only one hand steers; two hands on the wheel of a four-seater do not both register (user, 2026-10-19 night). Haiku fixer + verifier (CockpitControls, DrivingWeapons, GameInputBridge). Done in code (2026-10-19, Haiku fixer, merged and built): causes in the code: the rim was tested against the wheel transform's origin with a fixed 12 cm plane (a rim set back from it was out of reach), only a squeeze made at the rim took it (a second hand arriving with its grip shut never registered), and two hands averaged their targets (a resting hand dragged the wheel back). Now the rim is measured from the wheel's own mesh (the old origin and 20 cm when unreadable; the log says "Steering wheel ... rim measured"), a grip closed on the way takes it, the moving hand steers and the other is carried along. To check in the headset: a four-seater with both hands.

300. [ ] Scopes: a scope did not work on a gun with a laser (name not remembered), and racking it was not clean (user, 2026-10-19 night). Haiku fixer + verifier (VRScope, ScriptKeys laser). Done in code (2026-10-19, Haiku fixer, merged and built; the lead checked the new name filters over the export: none of the 556 slide, handle, bolt and magazine parts in use is taken out by them): the cause found: a laser toggle whose script is named after its sight ("Laser Sight") matched the Magnifier words before the Laser words, so the aim press (and the optic grab) tapped the laser and the scope never switched; Laser is now checked first (also 镭射, 激光), and a script with a generic name is classified by the word nearest its key check. 301. [ ] Sight switching by hand: a flip magnifier (in front of an EOTech or any switchable sight) is held by the hand and flipped by the hand's movement, not switched by a button press that applies it on its own (user, 2026-10-19 night). Haiku fixer + verifier. Done in code (2026-10-19, Haiku fixer; its worktree started 532 commits back, rebased and merged by the lead, built): the free hand, empty, grips the magnifier part (found by name: magnif, flip, 3x, g33, ftc, unity; never the main optic) within the grab radius; sliding it 4 cm across the bore or rolling it 35 degrees taps the weapon's Magnifier key once (a click); the hand comes back before the next flip; letting go never flips; shots re-base it. 302. [ ] CZ Bren 2 family: the rack (user, 2026-10-19 night, while testing: "concentrate the bughunt on cz bren 2 ... and their racks. make sure they work"). Offline over the export (all six variants analyse alike): the moving part is "Charging Handle/Bolt" (the fire animation's, inside the receiver); its parent "Charging Handle" is a static 38 cm mesh on the left of the receiver with the L-shaped knob 4.5 cm out at the front; the rack point is on the knob's tip (10 cm ahead of the bolt), the stroke 11.5 cm straight back; the 3 cm carve takes exactly the whole knob and nothing else, and the knob's path back is clear of every part (3.5 cm at the nearest); the Tac's magnifier grip (task 301) takes "Eotech G33/Magnifier", 20.6 cm from the knob (no conflict). Magazine (not the rack): its line leans 10-15 degrees back from the well (mag_line_scan: 53-78 points beside the outline half way in; a 10-15 degree forward tilt clears it); not the top cartridges (left out, the lean stays and a sideways tilt comes in), so the open question of 194 (to judge in the headset). Code trace of the rack path: Haiku workflow (four tracers, a skeptic per finding), results below. Code trace (Haiku workflow: four tracers, a skeptic per finding; 9 confirmed of 17): (a) locked open after the last round the knob is drawn at home while the grab zone and the stroke start sat 11.5 cm back with the bolt: the knob was hardly in reach where drawn and jumped back from the hand on the grab, and a tap on it chambered with no pull; (b) the rack point was snapped 3.2-3.6 cm inboard of the knob (BoltInsideOnSurface had asked for the surface snap before the knob was found); (c) about 6 cm off the knob tip toward the handguard the support grip won over the knob; (d) on the Tac the magnifier grip (301) gave way to the raw analysed point, 3 cm off the knob. Done in code (built): (a) a made knob locked forward (KnobLockedAlone, no bolt of its own) is taken at home, the bolt stays on its catch while it is worked and is let go only from a full pull (as an AR's handle with its bolt locked); (b) the knob keeps its own point; (c) a palm in the handle's zone is the handle's unless clearly nearer the handguard; (d) the magnifier grip weighs the reload's own rack zone. (e) the knob was in no hand solid (the fingers closed on the bolt's place inside the receiver): it takes the drawn-handle bar now. To check in the headset: rack, fire the magazine empty, reload and rack from the lock, on the Bren 2 BR and Tac.
303. [ ] FNX-45 in front of the view while a primary is held, sometimes; may happen with every pistol (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing; not tested further). Likely cause (offline, the export): the holstered copy (the weapon's third-person model) carries parts it should not draw: the FNX-45 laser variants' 'laser_beam' mesh, reaching out of the holster, and the RDS variants' 'Sight (1)' parked 27 cm in front of the pistol, which stretched the copy's box: its barrel axis, centre and length come from that box, so the copy was turned and placed wrongly, and the test that hides a copy in front of the eyes measured the wrong centre. Done in code (built): the template the holstered and dropped copies are made from leaves out beams and light cones by name, and clusters of parts standing more than 5 cm apart from the gun's body when small (under 40% of the body) or parked far (farther than half its size) and not as big as it (an akimbo pair's second gun stays). Over the export (tools/export_checks/stray_parts_rule.py): the FNX laser beams and RDS sight, parked shells and loaders (R870, M1014, 76 Eagle), the Garand's clip, the PKM / PKP / Type 80 loose belt links; the akimbo pistols, the blunderbuss and the C4 keep everything. To check in the headset: an FNX-45 (RDS, laser) in its holster while a primary is held.
304. [ ] mwii-m4 Holo (No Script) and its family: the charging handle can be grabbed and pulled, but nothing goes back (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing). Cause (offline, the export): their charging handle is one part, its rod and T together ('对象001_001', 17.3 x 5.6 x 1.8 cm, on the bore line, its back end at the receiver's rear); ArOwnHandleNoSlide only took a short flat T (3-12 cm long, 1.5 cm tall), so nothing was taken and only the bolt carrier inside moved (meshes graphics-card only: the T cannot be cut out). Done in code (built): a whole handle is taken too (12-22 cm along the bore, its T 3 cm+ across, 2.2 cm tall at most, on the bore line under the sights, back end within 6 cm of the guess), the hand takes it 1.2 cm in from its back end, and the bolt carrier is still taken and moves with it (ArBoltCarrier now runs after a handle found with no slide). Checked over the export (tools/export_checks/ar_long_handle_rule.py): the same limits pick exactly the handle the analysis knows by name on every AR that has one, and change only the three MWII M4s. To check in the headset: the T comes back with the hand, the carrier moves in the port, the T stays forward when the bolt locks open on an empty magazine.
305. [ ] QJB 95 and its drum version: cannot be racked (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing: "maybe hitbox issue"). Cause (offline, the export): the cocking hook under the carry handle is part of the moving part's 29 cm rod; the analysis puts the rack point on such a part's tip farthest from the bore by its vertices, but the 95 family's meshes are on the graphics card only, so it stayed on the bolt tube inside the receiver, 19 cm behind the hook (beyond the 11.7 cm reach). Done in code (built): for a moving part the analysis could not read, HeldWeapon measures the part alone from three sides (depth, shared per type) and takes the rack point from its drawn surface point farthest from the bore on the receiver (WeaponAnalyzer.WantsDrawnSlideTip / TipOfSlide). Offline (tools/export_checks/slide_tip_rule.py): the 95-1 / QJB 95 family's point moves 19.6 cm onto the hook; it also moves onto the side tab on the AK-12 squad family, AK-24s, AK-74M Zenit and MWII AK-105s (6-7 cm), the Grease Gun (12 cm) and the Ingram M10 (5 cm). To check in the headset: the 95s and AKs rack from their handles (log: "rack point on its moving part's drawn tip").
306. [ ] Hitting things with held objects (a weapon swung into something) does no damage (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing). Looked at: a gun swung into an enemy does hit (ThrowAndMelee.Bash, since 2026-10-16), but only its muzzle's tip or its butt was swept, and only above 3.1 m/s there: a swing landing with the middle of the gun, or a pistol punch (its muzzle moves about as fast as the fist), never counted. Done in code (built): four points along the gun (butt to muzzle) are swept, each with its own speed, from the knife's 2.6 m/s (MeleeSwingSpeed). Not done: hits with a magazine, a grenade or the bare hand (only held guns and melee weapons hit). To check in the headset: punch an enemy with a pistol, butt-stroke with a rifle (log: "swung into ..."). 2026-10-20 night (e0ce179): a magazine, shell or rocket in a hand, or a grenade held to throw, now hits too (MeleeHandler.HandBlows: the palm's path, the same speed rule, lighter blows, credited to the gun in use). The bare hand still does not. To check in the headset: swing a magazine into an enemy (log: "a held magazine: swung into ...").
307. [ ] Some bolts do not go back: not the part pulled to rack, the bolt seen in the ejection port (where the cases come out) (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing; guns not named). Looked at offline (2026-10-20 night, tools/export_checks/still_bolts.py: nodes named as a bolt or carrier outside the slide's, bolt's and handle's subtrees, over the export): 17 guns. Real ones: (a) the MG3s: 'mg3_bolt' (two meshes, under the cover, seen with it open) is a group apart from 'mg3_charginghandle', which is the moving "slide": racked, the handle comes back, the bolt stays (an open bolt rests back when cocked, so it should end back). (b) The K13 family (7 variants): the parent '416F BOLT' has a mesh of its own that is the whole charging handle, T and shaft (19 cm along the bore, 6 cm across at the T, 0.8 cm thick; drawn), and the mod moves only its child '416F ZG' (a 3.5 x 4 cm latch on the T) as "the handle": the latch comes back, the T stays (as 304's MWII M4s). Not fixed blind (the analysis's handle, slide and carve paths interlock; the game is needed to check): for the K13, take the parent's own mesh as the carve source (CarveFromPath, AR rule) instead of the latch; for the MG3, move the bolt group with the slide. False alarms: the BRN180s' 'Bolt_low.002' (a handguard screw, 8.6 cm tall, under the handguard), Suzuya's AR-15 'Bolt cover' (the dust cover), the akimbo P220s (the second pistol's slide moves, the first's not: akimbo). K13 fixed after all (e1448a9): ArStaticHandle takes a slide's holder that draws an AR charging handle of its own (12-25 cm along the bore, 1.5 cm tall at most, 3 cm+ across; parent_handle_rule.py: the seven K13s and Suzuya's two AR-15s, nothing else) as the mesh the AR rule cuts the T out of (CarveFromPath), instead of a latch among its children. To check in the headset: a K13's T comes back with the hand (the latch on it stays), the carrier moves in the port; Suzuya's AR-15 racks as before, with no hole left in the receiver. The MG3's bolt still open. MG3 fixed too (f49f977): BoltBesideHandle makes a bolt group that is the sibling of a slide named as a charging handle its companion (over the export: the two MG3s alone), so it comes back with the handle when racked (it goes home with it too, though an open bolt would rest back). To check in the headset: MG3 cover open, rack: the bolt comes back with the handle.
308. [ ] Picking up a weapon: the weapon given up in exchange spawned on top of the one being picked up and knocked into it; it should drop at the player's feet (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing). Done in code (built): the weapon given up drops at the player's feet, a little to the weapon side and forward, lying across the body (WeaponDrops.PickUp spawned it 10 cm over the picked-up weapon). To check in the headset.
309. [ ] A hitch of about half a second, sometimes, when killing someone (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing). Looked at (no game): the menu warm-up had prepared every weapon's drop model and shape this session (the log: 56 weapons), so not a cold model. Found on the death-frame path: the dropped copy's shape was kept per scale to three decimals, and a soldier's hand-bone scale wobbles there (0.999 / 1.000), so drops could miss it and re-cook a dozen convex colliders on the death frame; and a rendered shape that finished measuring after the warm-up's bake was cooked on the first drop. Done in code (built): drop scales snap to 1 within 1.5% (else to 1%), a rendered shape is cooked on the main thread as soon as it is measured, and a drop taking over 20 ms on the death frame is logged ("Weapon drop of X took N ms") so the next hitch shows whether the drop is the cause. To check: play, kill, look for that line. 2026-10-20, the user's session of the night before (reports/shots/play-1020): four drops logged, K13 Spectre 83 ms, QBZ192 47 ms, QJB201 Mag 297 ms, mk18riii 65 ms, all "shape fitted". The match had started while the menu's warm-up was still on its last pass (99 of the weapons; it pauses in a match), so the K13 Spectre, QBZ192 and mk18riii were cold: their template was built and their shape read and fitted on the death frame (the mk18riii's template log line comes right before its drop). The QJB201 Mag was prepared and still took 297 ms (a garbage collection is the likely cause; not known yet). Done in code (7dc28f2): a shape never fitted is no longer fitted on the death frame: the copy lies on a box from its drawn bounds, its points are read the next frame, the hulls are fitted on a worker thread, cooked three a frame and swapped in on every copy of it. The log line now gives the parts: the model's copy (and whether it built its first template), the shape, the colliders, and whether a garbage collection ran. To check: play, kill; the line shows which part is left. From the review of it (a Haiku reviewer, 2026-10-20; afdbf4a): the held gun's stand-in judges the swapped colliders afresh (it had the drop marked as resting by its rigidbody), a fit from before a content reload is dropped, the interim box is a plain BoxCollider (no hull mesh left behind). Also (36a1698) the menu warm-up takes the bots' team pools right after the loadout, and the autotest checks a drop of a type with its shape forgotten: a box, then its own shape within 3 s (7f8310c). A first drop at a scale other than 1 takes the late path too (35b4168).
310. [ ] HK416 family: the magazine still goes in and comes out wrongly, a little through the gun (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing: "look at the animator"). Cause (offline, the export: tools/export_checks/mag_line_scan.py, a sweep of the line's lean): the analysed lines lean back toward the trigger guard: HK416 -18 degrees (the curved-magazine "top tangent" is swung by vertex density: the top round's dense mesh against the walls' few vertices; area-weighted it reads -6), M4A1 -15, CZ Bren 2 -13.5 (its well wants -27), P320 +7.6 (wants -12..-15). Along each the magazine, still in the well, stands out of the grip's face: 85-553 points; at the best lean 4-32 (the HK416 bottoms out at 231, its curve). The runtime fit (task 270) counted only points inside the gun's walls, never those in the air beside the well. Done in code (built): the fit also counts a magazine point hidden in the well when seated that, still above the mouth, leaves the gun's side silhouette or goes out through a side (GunVolume.InSideSilhouette, the side view), as the offline scan does; the tilt search (24 degrees either way) and its keep-only-when-clearly-better rule are as before; and a pistol whose magazine cannot be read takes its drawn skin's long axis first (task 311). To check in the headset: the log line "magazine line fitted to the drawn gun, turned N deg" on the HK416s, M4A1s, CZ Bren 2s and P320s, and the magazine going straight up the well. Again 2026-10-20 night (Tasks 321): the scan's lines were themselves partly through the walls (a point pushed into the gun counted as hidden); the table now comes from mag_line_body.py --both (walls and out of the outline together): the HK416 family +12 (wall 54 against 437 analysed, 211 at the scan's +7.5), HK416-C -10.5, HK416-D +12 (left out before). And the capture's straight 0.08 s ease from up to 4.5 cm aside is routed under the well now (2ce7887, 995a0fa). To check in the headset.
311. [ ] P320 (and maybe more pistols): the magazine still goes through the grip on the way in; the CZ Bren 2 too, a little ("why didnt you fix it on it?") (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing). See 310 (the same cause and fix); the P320's magazine is on the graphics card only, so its line was the knuckle guess (18 degrees off its own axis): its drawn skin's axis is taken first now. Offline over the 15 pistols whose magazines are on the graphics card only (points out of place on the way in, analysed line / magazine's own axis): GLOCK 43X and 43X-S 948 / 1, 43X FA 803 / 11, P320 587 / 216, but MWII G17s 16-20 / 52 and S-IND7 20 / 24: so the skin's axis is taken only when the fit's own count says it is clearly better (at most 60%, 6 points fewer), then the tilt search as before. 2026-10-20, the offline sweep over the other 27 self-loading pistols (scratchpad mag_line_sweep.py, points out of place on the way in along the analysed line and at the best lean): 21 have an analysed line too upright for their grip's rake, and the runtime fit (as emulated: tilt to 24 degrees, kept when clearly better) turns all 21: P320 and P320s 614 -> 32 (lean +7.6 -> -13.4), GLOCK 43X family 809-977 -> 0, P220 family 547-583 -> 13-53, S-IND7 20 -> 12, SF/Springfield 1911 family 237 -> 81, Q1911 25 -> 0, SpringField 1911 T 71 -> 0, Suzuya's PX9105 123 -> 0. None left clearly better elsewhere. So the pistols depend on the runtime fit running (once the gun's and the magazine's volumes are measured, a few frames after the draw); the log line "magazine line ... taken from its drawn shape" or "fitted to the drawn gun" shows it did. To check: P320, GLOCK 43X, P220 in the headset, and their log lines. Again 2026-10-20 night (Tasks 321): the P320 -15/-6 (its magazine sits canted in the model: from the front, -6 sideways follows it; wall 25 against 574 analysed), the CZ Bren 2s +15 (the scan's -12 went the wrong way: 2178 points in the walls; +15 comes straight down, 3), and the capture routed under the well instead of eased straight through the grip's corner (2ce7887, 995a0fa). To check in the headset.
312. Done in code (built): FindBeltParts also takes an unnamed belt (touching the box, across the receiver over the bore's line, under the cover, smaller than the box, not a tray, feed, cover, release, trigger, stock, grip or magazine part): hidden while no belt is laid (box out, cover lifted), shown where it is modelled once laid, and the mod's strip is not drawn on such a gun (the held strip from the box mouth still shows while laying). 314. [ ] Cannot shoot while running (sprinting) (user, 2026-10-19 night, playing). Cause: the game lowers the weapon and does not fire while sprinting, and the VR toggle sprint stayed on as long as the stick was pushed. Done in code (built): the gun hand's trigger pulled with a weapon drawn ends the sprint (toggle) or holds it off (hold mode). To check in the headset: sprint, pull the trigger: the gun comes up and fires after the game's short recovery. 2026-10-20 (user: "still also i cannot shoot while running"): the game's own switch for it is FpsActorController.allowFireWhileSprinting (WantsToFire drops the trigger while sprinting and for 0.2 s after unless it is set). Done in code (0c400be): it is set on the VR player's controller, the trigger no longer ends the sprint, and the weapon's sprint tuck (its animator's "tuck", a lowered pose that also holds the fire animation back) is kept off in VR. To check: sprint and shoot. The autotest now sprints and holds the trigger half a second (c7581d5: "fire while sprinting").
315. [ ] "Many visual bugs and mags flying around" on pistols, it all works but is still buggy sometimes (user, 2026-10-20). Not specific: which pistols, and whether the magazines fly while held, when dropped, or when knocked out by a fresh one, still to ask. Found (2026-10-20, code): the hands' velocity, which every magazine, gun or grenade let go takes, added the rig's motion as the motion of the play area's ORIGIN, and the user stands about 1.6 m from it (the log's head position): a smooth turn of 120 deg/s moved the origin 3.4 m/s sideways while the hands moved about 1, a small snap turn gave a one-frame jump of up to some 40 m/s, and the view shake of every shot (the rig turned up to 0.8 deg about the eyes) gave about 2 m/s of jitter for a few frames: a pistol emptied and its magazine dropped at once flew off. Fixed (ebbf193, then the shake left out): the rig's motion is taken at the hand from the rig's pose before the shake, and a snap turn is a jump, not motion. The held gun's physics stand-in also jumps with a snap turn now (537ce6e; driven, it swept some 25 cm at up to 25 m/s through whatever lay by it). The autotest checks a still hand's velocity after a view shake, a 15 deg snap turn and in a 120 deg/s smooth turn (d232abe). The user's config: TurnMode Smooth at 120 deg/s. Also (2026-10-20 evening): the stand-in follows the gun without each shot's view shake (ba15670) and lets loose props it lands in after a snap jump pass through (da2e26b); what is let go keeps the rig's turn as spin, and the break-action wrist flick reads only the controller's own spin (5bed53f); the flung log judges speed across and upward, never by falling (11c5d91). Also done: a dropped magazine flying off far faster than it was let go is now logged once ("Magazine of X flung: ... let go ... by ...; near it: ...", 9454749), so the next session shows which path flings them and what pushed them; a fire-mode variant's state could say its magazine was in while the parent's was in the hand, so the magazine parts came back into the gun beside the prop (23c5f89; 249 item, held); the pistol line sweep above (311). One cause found 2026-10-20 night (flung-magazine hunt, Tasks 321): the gun in the hand is a physics stand-in that follows the hand by velocity (up to 25 m/s, no give); only magazines lying still were ignored, so a falling or just-dropped one it met (an ejected magazine dropping past the gun, one let go beside it) left at the hand's speed or more. Fixed (1bbd91b): loose magazines never meet the held gun, from the frame they drop (dropped weapons still bounce off). Also this night: the capture's straight ease through the grip routed under the well (2ce7887, 995a0fa). Not changed: an ejected magazine keeps the hand's velocity (a flick throws it, walking carries it). To check in the headset.

Holsters and pouch (451daa7): the torso's facing blends in the head's up as the head pitches toward the floor or the sky; looking almost straight down the old fallback took the up the wrong way round and turned them 145 degrees behind the back, and a tilted head there swung them up to 84 degrees. Input hunt (1f52f79): Touch's double A opened the pause menu too (it is the lower button's double press), an unknown controller type made every A press switch slow motion (a failed query now keeps the known type, unknown counts as Touch), the Vive wand's held trigger never left a seat (its Use is a complex button; the pulled trigger counts now), a stick held down through a death crouched the respawned soldier.
