Open: the Panzerfaust 3's rocket is its stock 铁拳3 枪托 (weapon report: "magazine pull pointed into the gun (turned around)"; its warhead 弹药 is not drawn at rest in the export): hand-loaded, a stock comes out of the pouch; to look at with the game. The user's "mag glitches through the handle a little but near" (pistols, then the M4A1s), two causes found and fixed. (1) Insertion: a magazine is taken onto its line from up to 4.5 cm aside and 40 degrees turned at the well's mouth (a pistol's grip), then eased straight onto the line in 0.08 s with no check against the gun: it cut through the grip's or magwell's bottom corner (insertion hunt). It now goes round under the well (2ce7887): its top down below the mouth along the line, straightened, across under the well, then up the line (0.14 s; simulated: its top face stays 3 mm under the mouth while off the line); the same both ways (995a0fa): pulled 20 cm aside from inside the well it comes out along the line first. (2) The magazine-line table itself: the scans count a magazine point pushed INTO the gun as hidden, so the "best" lines drove magazines through the well's walls. New offline check tools/export_checks/mag_line_body.py (points on the gun's body off the magazine's own seated footprint, side and front views; --both adds the scans' out-of-the-outline count and searches the tilt for the least of both): 37 of the scan table's 70 lines went through more wall than the analysed line (the 1911s 0 -> 1581, P220s 132 -> 903, CZ Bren 2s 885 -> 2178 when +15 gives 0, AK-12 squads 0 -> 362, AF2011, QBZ192/191, Suzuya's AR-15). Checked in pictures (mag_line_draw.py, now with a front view): Colt M1911, PPSh-41 (its -16.5 pulled the top block through the magazine catch: set by hand to +6.5, straight down the gun), CZ Bren 2 (+15 comes straight down), P220, P320 from the front (canted in its model: -6 sideways follows it), BRN180 (-6 right), M417A2 (an uneven magwell bottom counts on every line: left out). Table regenerated from --both over all 288 guns (b9e73c0): 101 lines, detachable magazines only, none on the search's edge unchecked (the Vectors and Glock 19 keep -16.5), each through no more wall than the analysed line; M4A1 now +13.5..+15 (wall 0-104 against 423-521), HK416 +12, new: FNX-45s, mwii-g17s, BRN180s, AS Vals, K13s, APC45, MP9 PDW, HK416-C, QBZ192s. Hunts this stretch (Haiku): vehicles (14db07c: turrets hold still under a panel's laser; a seat's drawn weapon put away when drawing there stops), visual (fdb0edc: one lens-flash limiter decision for both eyes, Tasks 225's "one eye brighter"; the glow at the plume), throws and melee (7ab9218, 1caf49e, 13b7882: pinless throwables live from the free hand, a throw scales only the hand's swing (a car's 60 m/s went 84), a seated grip throw puts the grenade away, a pulled pin kept through a hand-over, a tracking jump no swing), drops (562324c: the swapped weapon kept before the cull, stretched drop shapes keyed per axis). Open: the scope shadow's side (visual hunt says the crescent should come from the far side; check in the headset), the Panzerfaust 3's stock-rocket (above). Later still: lifecycle (4890e65: a rig whose construction threw left its depth-100 camera over the flat game, carved receivers kept across content reloads, handlers left subscribed when the graphics presets threw, pause flags and the other hand's weapon on shutdown), still bolts offline (8e987db, Tasks 307: MG3 bolt, K13 handle; logged, not fixed blind), the held gun's stand-in flung loose magazines (1bbd91b, Tasks 315: now never met), two-handed aim (c6ae269: no steer from a hand behind the grip, faded 3-8 cm instead of a cut at 5, the steadying restarted on a snap turn). Checked and left: an ejected magazine keeps the hand's velocity (a flick throws it); the stand-in's 25 m/s chase (capping it makes fast swings lag on contact); the draw's 25 animator steps (a few ms; an early stop risks showing the draw clip on some guns); the scope shadow's side (to check in the headset).
322. [ ] From the user's play of build f49f977 (2026-10-20 night): "m4a1 familys, i think the mag thingy is fixed but now i cannot pull back the bolt thingy again". Found two causes (no rack lines in the log, so from the code and the export). (a) The magazine-line table (first shipped after 8c8eb56) was written in the export's glTF space (x, y, -z) and applied as Unity vectors: every line mirrored in z, 134-178 degrees off the analysed one (the log), the magazines' tip and base swapped and the seated magazine's grab zone (its 'base') at its top under the receiver, beside the charging handle, on the M4A1 m4/ACOG/CCO/EOT and the other 95 table guns. Fixed (14e7093): z negated, each line now its tilt off the analysed one, and a line more than 25 degrees off the game's own analysis is not taken. (b) The M4A1 [SQUAD] family (the user's rifle in that session) draws its charging handle whole ('对象003', T and shaft, 16.3 cm), and the T rule took only 3-12 cm parts: the hand racked the carrier inside the receiver and the T stayed. Fixed (fae2f76): a second pass takes a whole flat handle where no short T was found (whole_t_rule.py: the three squad M4A1s). To check in the headset: M4A1 [SQUAD] and M4A1 (ACOG, CCO, EOT): the T comes back with the hand, normally and after the last round; their magazines out and in along the well. Also open from that log: drops of the PKP, PKM and Type 80 still cost 250-300 ms on the death frame (model built at the first drop, not on the loading screen). Then (work-until-limit 85 / weekly 14, the user gaming, the game not started; each built and installed): the kill lag's remaining part (2b66db6: the loading screen queues all 286 pool types and ends with most unbuilt, so what is left is built while dead, deploying or in a menu, the types living soldiers carry first), review follow-ups (32493b3: torso facing from the head's forward only, kept past 72 degrees of pitch; the slow-motion A blocks its slide-release mode switch 1.2 s; shutdown undoes the mod's own pause), hands (0a61d3a: a glove shown again takes its finger goals at once), a grip throw in a seat with its own personal weapons holsters the grenade (dd5df60), the held weapon's lenses and counter destroyed first on release (3206901), a magazine dropped inside the player's vehicle falls through that collider instead of being thrown out (541fcd0), launchers: a stock is never the rocket and 弹药/弹头/炮弹 name one (6b65547, the Panzerfaust 3), casings led by a frame of hand motion (8d86ced), drops kept when the game spawns nothing (a4ce54e), and tools/export_checks/mag_line_table_check.py (13f10f8: the table against the export in Unity's space; it flags all 101 lines of the mirrored table). Rack trace (Haiku, read-only) then found two more for every M4 shape (316b8c3): (c) the support hand slid back from the handguard onto the T with its grip still closed stayed on the handguard (the hold lets go only at 60 cm, and a rack needs a fresh squeeze): it now takes the handle when the handle is well within its zone and clearly nearer than the handguard line; (d) the sight-switch zone (from 5 cm behind the grip along the sight line) lay over the T and a palm under it took the sight switch: it gives way where the handle is about as near. The MWII M4's handle and carrier are siblings (no double travel). To check in the headset: hold the handguard, slide the hand back onto the T without letting go, pull. Open from the same log: in play (OnFoot) the input check's frame rate read 31-44 fps (85-89 in the menus and the follow view): a perf pass with -rfvr-perf when the game may be run (the ACOG's lens camera, the bots' drops, the hands' volumes are the first suspects). More this run (each built, installed): idle drop builds only for the types the living soldiers carry, a slow one at most once a second (a menu stuttering in the headset was worse); review and hunt follow-ups: magazines dropped in a vehicle fall through its hull, the scope's shadow from the side away from the eye, two-handed aim eased out over 0.33 s on a tracking loss, drop reach measured again after the first physics step, in-flight gun-volume places given back once, launchers' stock never their rocket, a pistol's thumb floored at its measured pose; autotest SupportSlideSelfCheck (the support hand slid onto the handle with its grip held must take it). Confirmed from the user's log: all 34 'magazine line from the offline table, N deg' readings (133.8-177.7) equal what the old table gives against the export's line in Unity's space to 0.1 degree, so the game analyses these guns as the export says and the mirror was the whole fault; with the fix each reads its tilt (4.5-16.5 degrees), under the new 25-degree guard. The K13's T is cut in a 3.85 cm sphere (CarveRadius; its wings reach 3.3 cm). Later the same night (builds 7f178b6, 7ffbcec, then the input-check timing): the death-frame drops of 235-300 ms were the whole weapon analysis run only for the parked parts the copy hides (the warm-up had reached 78 of 286 weapons); those parts are now kept on disk per prefab (ParkedPartsCache, BepInEx/cache/RavenfieldVR-parked-parts.txt, hashed against the prefab's transforms and written again by every analysis), so from the second session on a first drop needs no analysis, and the drop log says how much of a slow first template its analysis was. The idle builder's shape step could never run (a built type went back in line for good); fixed, and it now also analyses the types whose model took the cache (a pickup then waits for nothing). Sound hunt: the caller's volume never reached the gun's own or donor sounds (the light switch played the whole rack sample at full volume, the slide-lock click too); reload grabs blocked with the pause menu up; the release's audio object destroyed earlier. Perf hunt: no per-frame garbage left on the hot paths (the once-logs are all gated), the body-shadow call is a few renderer flags; the input check now logs the compositor's GPU ms against the frame interval, so the next session's log tells whether 31-46 fps in play is the GPU (the Ultra preset: render scale 1.4, MSAA 4x, 4 km view) or the CPU. Open: an en-bloc clip pressed home with the bolt locked cuts its own MagIn slice with the bolt's (one slice source; only when MagIn is a reload-clip slice). Then (builds 9829ee8 to 0f3443f, all installed): the second weapon's worked action clicks a moment apart; the pouch label and magazine readout set their colour, plate and alpha only on a change; held magazines and grenades hit (task 306). Vehicles hunt: a support hand on a lit rifle's handguard no longer stops its trigger leaving the seat; the seat cycle skips seats past the seventh instead of ending; the trigger that entered a car must be let go once before it counts toward leaving (held, it threw the player out a second later); driving armed reset at VR shutdown. The GitHub audit (issues #1-#21, an automated scan of 2026-10-20): #7-#10 and #12-#14 were already fixed; fixed now (2f3fd46): pinned SHA-256 for the downloaded tools (#2/#17, checked against GitHub's and nuget.org's own hashes), manifest paths and zip entries kept inside the game folder (#3/#4), the self-repair's runtime files checked against the XR plugin's hashes (#5), ucrtbased.dll no longer shipped (#6: neither native DLL imports it), a failed VR start stops what it created (#11/#16), the Steam config reader's bounds (#15), #19-#21; open: #18 (a test project and CI). Hunt of today's code (0f3443f): GunVolume places released on cache clear and eviction, a drop shape that cannot be prepared given up, a re-grab clears the magazine's left-over rail switch, the parked-parts cache retries a failed write. Rig hunt (later build): a crouch or prone toggle while physically crouched lowers the view to the deeper of the two drops, never both (with prone it went below the ground); the crouch compensation reset on every mode change; a stick held down into the menu no longer toggles prone on resume. Checked and left: Calibrate now accepts any height on purpose (seated play); the snap latch clears on the first frame after a menu; turning while the dashboard pauses the game (SteamVR holds the input then, not verified). To check in the headset: crouch physically, then flick the stick down (crouch): the view stays at your head; hold it down (prone): the view goes to the prone height, not below it. Scope shadow: 21ea236 (this evening) had turned it to come in from the side away from the eye on a reversed derivation; put back on the eye's own side (behind the exit pupil an eye moved left sees the right of the field). To check in the headset: move the eye off a scope's line to the left: the dark crescent comes in from the left, and moving right clears it. The en-bloc slice cut is done too (two slice sources, 0c64bae). Second-weapon hunt (e627925, 6c029e7; built, installed once the game is closed): the free hand's pistol kept the main gun's grip class (a rifle's, written over it every frame); it reloads in Assisted mode too; an empty pull of its trigger clicks on its own hand; it is put away on a scene change before the ammo states are cleared. Left: a pump or bolt gun in the free hand still plays its fire animation's cycle as well as the hand's (to check in the headset). 2026-10-21 morning (work-until-limit 85 / weekly 30; the user away, the game free to test): the last session's log (mod ms a frame, new) showed the mod at 0.4-0.5 ms in play early, 1-3 ms later in the match (per-kill late shape fits: a main-thread gather and up to 3 hull cooks a frame), 2-8 ms dead in the follow view (the idle builder's analyses, 25-270 ms once a second) and 6-23 ms on the loading screen (the drop builds, by design). Fixed: the idle analyses only on the respawn or loadout screen (90358d3). Checked and clean: the Harmony patches on per-soldier hot paths (Weapon.Update, Shoot, CanFire, SoundBank, AimTransform, CharacterController.Move: a few null checks before the player test), GunCollider's player-collider gathering. Running: a quick autotest with the per-section profiler (-rfvr-perf) on the null driver. Then (all built, installed after the autotest): a pump or bolt second weapon's stroke after a shot only moves the rounds (its fire animation already cycled and sounded it); the input check logs the death-frame drops (count, avg, max ms); the loading screen runs the analyses the cache skipped (else the first pickup or draw of such a type paid 25-270 ms); threads hunt: GunVolume's hull settings set before any view renders (a synchronous read-back queued the decode first; latent on D3D11), OnReady volatile, a held gun's stalled shape measuring is retried as a failed one. Clean sweeps: the per-soldier Harmony patches, GunCollider's player colliders, the menu warm-up, holster drawing, ScriptKeys (ResolveCurrent is an event lookup), the loading view's resources, scope textures (pooled), config ranges, the free hand's grenade, the lens picture upload (only on a new picture). Ammo hunt (2026-10-21, nothing changed; for the user to decide): (1) a magazine stowed in the pouch, or despawning, at a full reserve loses what does not fit (ReturnSpareCapped, task 47's cap): pull a full 30 at a full reserve, stow it, and those 30 are gone; the cap could apply only to resupply. (2) LoseRoundOnRack is on by default: racking a loaded gun ejects its round as a casing, one round lost per rack (realistic; a setting). (3) A fire-mode variant keeps its own chamber flag apart from its parent's (unverified against the game; left). (4) A second weapon destroyed during its timed reload loses that reload's rounds (narrow; left). Also fixed from the threads hunt: a content reload restarts the shape fit of copies still on their box (f2e7731). Analyzer hunt (baa6d0a, 062c52c): a slide whose holder is scaled to nothing on an axis keeps its sampled rest (the division put it up to a million times the travel away); a not-finite magazine axis is not stored; a magazine's axis is taken from drawn meshes only (a disabled spare beside it leaned it). Grab-zone hunt (2e950d5): the optic is not taken where the handguard is about as near (a hand on top of the handguard switched the sight; the optic zone runs 30 cm along the sight line over it); an optic hold re-bases when the off hand comes back into tracking (the jump read as a slide and switched the sight). Left (design or low): the pouch takes a squeeze with an empty reserve (it says NO AMMO), the revolver cylinder opens only from its button, a squeeze and let-go on a locked-back slide chambers a round (as a slide release). Magazines through guns, measured (2026-10-21, autotest keep-out sweeps on the null driver): the quick autotest (0 problems) showed the JOLT-20-S's and PATRIOT GL's magazines ending 20-25 cm past the gun when pushed sideways, with up to 222 of the dense probe's points in the gun's body while the keep-out's own samples saw nothing. Fixed in 3ec7dea: nine points a row (corners too) and a long gun's magazine sampled to 95% of its edges (80% before): PATRIOT GL 58 frames/222 points -> 0/0, JOLT-20-S hard 12/54 -> 0/0, slow 65/70 -> 7/54, GREASER slow 58/132 -> 65/68. Next round running: rows at most 2 cm apart on a long magazine (the GREASER's 28.6 cm had 3.6 cm between rows).
323. [ ] The laser pointer's yellow ball does not touch the UI elements: it stops short of them, as if there were an invisible wall between (user, 2026-10-20 night, after the fixes of that evening; playing). Cause found (offline): menus are stacked toward the player by sorting order (CanvasManager.PlaceMenu, 4 mm a step, up to 20 cm), and the laser's ball sat on the nearest canvas rectangle it crossed, so a canvas sorted above the menu and clear where the laser pointed (a fader, a tooltip layer, a click blocker) was a wall centimetres in front. Done in code (10fda24, built; installed once the game is closed): the ball lands on the nearest canvas that draws something visible at that point (its groups' alpha included), else on the nearest rectangle as before; and a click goes to the first hit on a targeted canvas (UI hunt: a HUD canvas sorted above the menu could take it). Also from the UI hunt (ca57bdb): the SteamVR keyboard is hidden when its field goes. To check in the headset: point at the main menu, the options and the loadout: the ball sits on the buttons.
324. [ ] The support hand that grips the gun (the left for a right-hander) can still move freely away from where it gripped while it holds: it should stay on the grip until the grip is let go (user, 2026-10-20 night, playing: "if a player grips, dont make him able to move his hand anywhere else unless he let go of the gun"). Cause (offline): while the support hand held the handguard, its glove's snap pose was slid along the barrel to where the tracked hand was, every frame; and a gun whose support pose was missing or over 12 cm away left the glove on the tracked hand (only the fingers closed on the gun). Done in code (7945ea2, built; installed once the game is closed): the slide is taken at the grab and kept for the hold, and with no support pose the glove keeps its grab pose on the gun. It moves again when the grip is let go (or past the 60 cm yank). To check in the headset: take the handguard, move the hand along the gun and away from it: the glove stays where it gripped. Review of the fix (a96a5b1, installed before the test of 22:1x): a pistol's cup is not pinned (it settles onto the cup by its anchor), a grab made more than 5 cm off the handguard is not pinned (it pinned the glove in the air), the stretch also counts the hand's distance from its held glove (a pull along the barrel never let go), and the hold's latch is reset in Update too. Left: with two-handed aiming the gun still turns toward the tracked palm while the glove stays on the gun (the review called it inconsistent; the steering is the two-handed aim itself).
325. [ ] Some guns show a second visor in front of their sight, "like the vanilla in front of the original", the PATRIOT for one (user, 2026-10-20 night, playing). Cause (offline: the weapon report and the export's side view): the PATRIOT TAC has 2 sight modes, one of them narrower (the flat game's zoom, fov under 32), and the mod drew its own lens disc on that mode's line, at eye relief in front of the eye, though the model has only its red dot there. Done in code (built; installed once the game is closed): a narrower mode gets the mod's lens only when the model has an optic for it (parts the sight mode moves in, as the M417A2's magnifier, or a part named like a magnifier); otherwise the log says "no lens of the mod's is drawn for it". To check in the headset: the PATRIOT TAC in both sight modes shows only its red dot.
326. [ ] Bug-hunt and improve run of 2026-10-21 (work-until-limit 85 / weekly 30; the game started for null-driver tests while the user is away). (a) Weapon pickup hunt (376986a): a kept drop that fell out of the world is removed when its timer ends; the drop cap evicts the soldiers' drops before a weapon of yours; on death your kept and holster-bound drops become ordinary drops (they stayed beside the fresh loadout after respawn). (b) Magazine prop physics hunt, measured with a new autotest check (BuriedMagazineCheck: a magazine let go 15 cm and 40 cm under the ground, and a weapon 30 cm under it with a no-fix control beside it): before, both magazines fell through the world (top speed 14.8 m/s, ending 11 m under the ground: terrain and mesh ground are a surface, the push-out found nothing in them); after, both are put back on the surface along the line from the eyes (top speed 1.6 m/s, lying still). The same for dropped weapons, from the player's eyes or the dying soldier's chest (PropPhysics.NothingUnder / BackToward, shared). Also from that hunt: a magazine let go deeper than 25 cm in a wall was not moved out and PhysX flung it (now back toward the eyes); the bodies a dropped magazine ignores are gathered again on every drop (Unity forgets an ignored pair while the colliders are off in the hand, and the bodies remembered from the last drop were skipped); a knocked-out magazine's end-over-end spin is no longer cut at Unity's 7 rad/s; a held magazine destroyed with its scene frees the hand instead of throwing. Not changed: the release velocity's single-frame spike (no evidence the rig steps in one frame), 16 level colliders per drop (unmeasured), the stand-in's 0.1 s ignore gap (low). (c) The "magazine set down gently" check was skipped in every run (the drop checks before it had put the gun in its holster): it runs before them now and passes (0.0 cm). (d) Rig and locomotion hunt: a controller losing tracking keeps its tip and grip poses where they last were on it (the stand-in offsets differ, so a held gun stepped as tracking went and came back); a hand on a car door does not seat the player while the game is paused; seated by a hand at a door, the seat's view is used from that frame (one frame of the on-foot follow on a seated actor); a player gone between Update and the render leaves the view where it was (it went to the world's origin for a frame); the crouch-release timer runs on game time (a pause over 3 s just after standing let the view sink by up to 0.7 m); the intro's walker sort uses one key per walker (an inconsistent comparison could throw); the gun stand-in's physics step ignores a zero fixed step (the katana finisher's time stop). Not changed: smooth turning below 10 fps, the frame after a teleport, the vignette and damage-veil materials on a VR restart. (e) Tasks.md: 35 review items from earlier runs were already fixed in the code and only unticked; checked against the code and ticked. (f) Throw, melee and second-weapon hunt: while the match is paused with no menu (the SteamVR dashboard, the headset off) a grenade could be thrown (several in one pause), the knife and a swung gun hit and the free hand's gun fired: the gun-input block now includes the pause (GunInputBlocked), and the throw and blade guards use it. Not changed: the knife's trigger may add the game's own melee ray (needs a knife with ammo -1; unconfirmed), the blade can hit again on the return stroke after 0.28 s, the blade hits squadmates as the game's own melee does (the bash skips them), a dud that expires while the player is dead is not refunded, a throw a script takes spends its round. (g) Death-frame drop cost: the floor test runs from the drop's pivot, and the physics sync for its bounds only when there is no floor under it (the sync on every drop cost about 0.14 ms a drop). The profiler now names the weapon under test at each section's slowest call and the six slowest frames (PerfProbe.SetContext). (h) Looked at, not changed: a grenade's glove has 14/72 points inside it (the worst of the quick run): CentreGrenadeGrip puts its middle at the glove's palm centre, which is on the palm surface, so half of it is in the palm; a ball sits in the fist hollow, 2.6 cm in front of the palm and 3.4 cm toward the fingers, but the sideways part flips between the hands while the grip data is shared by both, so it needs an offset applied per hand at pose time (ThrowableHandler / SecondWeapon), measured with the FRAG check (points inside down, 4/4 fingers on it kept). (i) Vehicles hunt: while the match is paused with no menu the hands on the wheel and stick kept adding up their motion (the turn or stick throw landed at once on resume; now they move nothing and carry on from where they are), a held trigger still reached the game, and a grip on a holster drew or stowed a weapon, a seat weapon too, and a grip let go as the headset came off dropped the gun: all stopped while paused. Not changed: a seat-to-seat swap may place one frame with the old seat's rotation (hidden by the fade), the leave-seat hold across a VR restart. (j) Settings: the dropped magazine and weapon lifetimes and caps have ranges (1-3600 s, 1-100 / 0-100). (k) Slow motion survives a look at the dashboard or the headset taken off: the game's unpause set the time scale to 1 (slow motion is the time scale at 0.2), so it was switched off each time; the mod's resume puts the old speed back. (l) VR lifecycle hunt: the slow-motion key with the match paused by the mod (headset off, no menu) set the time scale and ran the match on (not forwarded while paused now); a level load unpaused the match but left the mod's pause flags set (reset on a scene change); an exception in the held gun's update skipped the free hand's weapon, the drops, the holsters and the grip to hold every frame (caught there now); the glove meshes were left behind by each rig rebuild; the dashboard state survived a SteamVR stop. Not changed: the katana finisher sets the time scale itself, a SteamVR crash without its quit event is not noticed, a WeaponSystem that fails half way leaks its parts. (m) Perf (profiler with context): the slowest frames (about 600 ms) come 1.2 s into the RPG-7s and a signal smoke, far above any mod section, so most likely the test's own pictures; the weapons' render step's 300 ms call was at the PKP 0.3 s after its draw, next to its box's first shape fit (8 pieces from 25 renderers' vertices): magazine props are now timed on their own ("magazine prop made"). A warm-up pre-fit of the magazine shape was written and taken out again before it shipped: the copy keeps only the parts active in the hierarchy, which a prefab asset (all the warm-up has) may not answer as a live weapon does, so it could have cached a different shape under the same key than the prop itself fits. (n) The autotest now prepares the weapons it tests as the menu's warm-up does (WeaponWarmup.Prepare; it starts a map at once, so the warm-up never ran) and starts the profiler after that: its numbers are a match after the menu, as in play, not each weapon's first analysis and fits. (o) A death-frame drop of an M4A1 took 32 ms with its model, shape and colliders at 0 ms and no garbage collection: the log line now also times ignoring the player and bodies, the under-the-ground test and the cull, to tell which. (p) Input hunt: a squeeze still took a magazine or a dud from the ground, pulled a grenade's pin or flipped a magnifier with the match paused and no menu (now gated on the pause too); an exception in the input bridge each frame left a weapon script's key tap held down for good (caught there now, the script keys still update); a toggled sprint stayed on through a menu and into the next life (cleared there); a failed haptic pulse built its log strings each time (latched per hand). After an input outage over 0.5 s (which clears the held buttons so none stays stuck) a button still down is no longer pressed again when the input comes back (from 2026-10-16: a grip took a second magazine, Pause / FireMode / Sprint toggled again). A trigger already held in the hand that just became the gun hand fires only after it is let go and pulled again (it fired at once). (The left-handed binding descriptions: done in (r).) (q) Grenade glove, measured on the FRAG: held by its middle at the palm centre 14/72 glove points in it; in the fist's hollow 2/72, four fingers on it either way; resting on the palm (the hollow plus the palm's skin and its half thickness) shipped: in the pictures the fingers now close round it, the grenade in the fist (before: hanging under the fist, its base out). (r) The Touch binding's description in SteamVR said B's hold was slow motion (it is A pressed twice since 2026-10-20): brought up to date; the left-handed layouts' descriptions now name the mirrored buttons (A/X, B/Y swapped for Touch and HP; 3 offline checks, 25 in all). (s) Two more profiler sections for first-time work: "shape fitted", "shape measuring started" and "bolt piece cut", to find the PKP's 300 ms (the magazine prop's own was 19 ms at most). (t) Optimize: the input layer's any-hand slot is the OR of the two hand reads instead of a third SteamVR call per action (17 of the 51 calls a frame; every binding is on a hand); measured against the Input section's 0.060 ms a frame in the next run. (u) Review of the day's own changes (a read-only reviewer over 376986a..HEAD and the uncommitted part): the instant fire-mode switch also fired for weapon scripts and longer variant cycles, which slow motion's double press then left switched (now only a switch a second one puts back exactly: the selector, or a gun with one variant beside it, cycled back; scripts, longer cycles and sight flips while aiming keep the 0.4 s wait); the pending put-back is cleared by a menu; the intro's new sort made a closure each frame (a static comparison now); the shortened switch time skipped a weapon whose fire-mode change is a reload (it finished that reload at once); a magazine deep in a wall is moved back toward the eyes at most a metre; the second weapon's grip pose is cleared whatever the hand does next. Kept as it is: the knife's swing is off while the laser is on a mod panel, as the gun's trigger is. (v) Holster hunt: after a steep look down (the body's facing frozen) and a turn, the first frame up swung the holsters and the pouch round the body by the whole turn at once (up to the other side), and a reach then landed on another holster: that catch-up now goes at most 150 degrees a second (normal head turns as before); the free hand's second weapon was drawn (and fired) in deep water and could 'draw' a toggled item, as the normal draw never does; a holster draw the game refused left the gun attached to the grabbing hand (it goes back to the hand that held it); a holster grab while driving drew night-vision goggles into the hand (switched now). Not changed: holster copies across scene loads (unconfirmed), invisible but grabbable holsters for weapons with no model, the free hand's weapon holstered with the main gun (looks intended). (w) Open, seen in the second-weapon check: with a launcher drawn (the SMAW, 'DAGGER'), a pistol laid at the free hand was not picked up, and the launcher moved to the other hand by TakeIntoHand was not taken up again within 2 s (no exception logged). The check now leaves launchers out; whether passing a launcher by its front grip works in play is still to be looked at. (x) Squad hunt: the pointing arrow aimed at the soldier of an older target order (the game never clears it; alive or dead) while the ground order went where the hand pointed: that soldier only while a target order is being made and alive; the hand-sign doc said 0.4 s where the code holds 0.5 s. A hand sign with no squadmates gives no order (it played the order's sound and grunt for nothing). Not changed (for a decision): the label and arrow do not follow target-order mode, the arrow ignores water and the 2000 m range the order uses, flags past the game's 400 m issue range can be ordered, a held sign may count again after a short tracking dropout, the loading blackout has no cap in the loading scene, GameContext's menu gate without a controller. (y) Final full autotest of the newest build (31 files, ~615 insertions; 6 problems) with a deep repro of the flagged weapons. Every measurement held or improved against the previous run (tools/metric_diff.py: frame time avg 10.47 vs 10.63 ms, Weapons.BeforeRender max 291 vs 366 ms; drops, shape, glove steady), and the previous run's two problems (the DAGGER launcher false alarm, the JOLT-20-S support slide) are gone. The six were two intermittent flakes that did not reproduce when the three weapons were hammered deep for 264 s: an RPG-7 / RPG-7V single-frame pose spike while held still (up to 75 deg in one of 17 samples; 0.00 deg in the deep run and in the previous run; the full run had a 562 ms frame hitch), and GREASER's "mag kick, slow push" leaving the fresh magazine unseated (not sampled for GREASER before, did not recur). Shown not to come from this change set: the only held-pose change (HeldWeapon.HoldFrame) is a no-op for anything over 20 cm (RPG is ~80 cm), and the reload/magazine diff is only the Create->CreateNow perf wrapper (same args). The deep run's own extra checks: GREASER let go 30 cm under the ground is laid out to -14 cm (from -1109 cm without the fix: an 11 m -> 14 cm gain, the weapon's shape still sinks a little), and RPG-7V did-not-fire / rocket-film / scope-bench (a single-shot launcher on the null driver). To do (both minor/pre-existing): the GREASER -14 cm sink, the RPG one-frame spike. (z) Two read-only Haiku scouts for the next rounds. Reload/magazine lifecycle: no critical/major; minor: a reload already in progress is not frozen while the match is paused (TryGrab is gated but the per-role switch in ReloadInteractions.Update still seats/kicks/stows and a rack still ejects) -- FIXED (the switch is skipped while paused, with the kick and rack baselines re-anchored on resume; guarded by IsPaused so normal play is unchanged); rounds are spent before the prop is made in six places with no refund if MagazineProp.Create throws (none known to throw); OutOfTheLevel's OverlapBox caps at 16 colliders -- FIXED (bumped to 32 so a magazine in a cluttered spot sees the walls past the first 16); BoltPiece's carve cache never Destroys its runtime meshes; an in-place sub-weapon switch keeps _kickPrevAlong/_kickPushSince -- FIXED (Release resets the mag-kick trackers); a palmed-magazine drop (ReloadInteractions.cs ReleaseProp) -- FIXED (guarded with Go == null -> Destroy, as the held prop already is) -- and the route anchor (MagazineProp.cs:197/234) have thin destroyed-object guards. Vehicle/seat: two majors, both from the one-frame-stale VRHand.Palm (set in BeforeRender, read in Rig.Update after physics moved the vehicle) -- the flight stick is measured against last frame's palm so at speed it pins to full throw, and the steering wheel's angle is world-space so the car's own turn counts as hand turn through a corner; plus a stale-palm grab reach that fails at speed, a hub grab with 1/r gain, and VRRig.cs:411-433 dereferencing actor without a null check -- FIXED (DetermineMode's actor-based branches are guarded with actor != null so a frame between death and respawn falls through to the camera follow; the dead check counts a null actor as dead). The two majors (flight stick / steering wheel stale palm) still need a headset check; logged for the bug-hunt rounds. (aa) A third scout audited the off-hand grenade, melee and optics for the same pause gaps the main hand got. Two majors, both in the off-hand grenade path (SecondWeapon) which the main-hand fixes did not reach, both FIXED: a trigger let go while the match was paused was thrown on resume (the block fed _triggerWas back, then the first frame back read the release as a throw) -- now _triggerWas is resynced on the unblock frame without firing, the pin staying out; and letting go of the off-hand GRIP while paused threw the grenade (LetGo checked GameMenuOpen, which a no-menu pause passes, but not GunInputBlocked) -- now gated like the main hand. Both guarded by GunInputBlocked, so normal play is unchanged. Minor/tiny the scout left for later: the pin-grab survives a pause -- pause part FIXED (UpdatePin ends the grab on pause, like MagnifierFlip) -- its pull test is still world-space (ThrowAndMelee.UpdatePin, body motion can pull the pin; tracking unchecked; both need a headset check), UpdateAim recomputes aim during a pause (haptic/visibility only), MagReadout shows during a pause (display only), ForgetPulledPins is death-only not scene-change, the off-hand pin pull has no visual.
327. [ ] From the user (2026-10-21, 18:15): (a) "fix also that the switch to full auto or semi has delays". Cause (code): on Touch the gun hand's A tap reaches the mod on the button's release (its long press is the FireMode action), and the switch then waited another 0.4 s for a possible double press; a fire-mode variant switched by the game also holds the trigger back for the weapon's changeFireModeTime (0.3 s by default), the flat game's selector animation, not shown in VR. Done in code: on Touch the gun hand's A switches at once (its double press is slow motion, never the menu; a semi / auto switch made by the first press of slow motion's double press is put back), other controllers keep the wait (their double press opens the menu); the player's own variant switch is ready after 0.05 s (WeaponFireModeSwitchPatch). To check in the headset: tap A on an automatic rifle: SEMI / AUTO shows at once and the next trigger pull fires in the new mode; A twice quickly still toggles slow motion and leaves the mode as it was. (b) "holding a object in the left hand as primary hand, or taking a pistol with the right hand while holding the weapon in the left hand or opposite around, the 2nd object sometimes isnt in the hand and floats in the air". Measured with a new autotest check (SecondWeaponFloatCheck: the gun in either hand, the second weapon drawn in the other, its drawn model's distance from that hand each frame for 1.5 s while the hand moves, with pictures; taken from its holster and picked up from the ground, the gun in the right and in the left hand). Found: the drawn weapon was always in the hand's place (within 0.3 cm), but the glove was not on it: (1) its shape was measured only once the game called it unholstered (the game's draw time, 1.2 s and more), so the measurement failed ("put away before every view was taken") and the hand had nothing to close on for up to 2 s (in a picture: an open glove beside the pistol); (2) left on the tracked hand, the glove grasped 4.3 cm off the weapon's grip (the gun hand's glove goes onto its weapon's own grip pose; the free hand's never did); (3) after each shot the kick moved the weapon out of the fist; (4) a switch of the main gun wiped the free hand's grip class (a pistol then held as a rifle); (5) a grenade first taken in the free hand hung from its throw animation's palm, not its middle; and the free hand's weapon played its whole draw clip. Done in code: measured from the first frame in the hand (GunVolume.For posedByHand), its draw clip skipped, the glove on its own grip pose (mirrored for a left hand) and carried with its kick, the main gun's release and support pose leave that hand alone, the grenade's middle as the grip.

[ ] From the user (2026-10-24, work-until-limit 85, the game not started): "i see alot of shjadoing and things flying around / lagging infront of my view if I use LOW, Medium high or ultra settings, on game settings it works fine. Also in general do performance improvements"; "I cannot grab people with my hand and throw them around with them taking damage when flapped against things or when thrown away too (but they dont die that much)"; "I also cannot punch them with parts of my weapons or my hands if they are fists. For these 2 feautures analyze boneworks code". Cause of the ghosting: the user's game quality is Fastest (no shadows, no pixel lights, no AA); every old preset added shadows (a depth pass and the casters again, per eye in multi-pass) and more, so the frame rate fell below the headset's and reprojection doubled what moved. Code done, not yet checked in the headset: presets retuned for multi-pass (cfaa7f7: Low no shadows, Medium hard to 30 m, High soft to 60 m, Ultra 100 m; render scales 0.8/0.9/1/1.2, migrated), 8-bit colour targets on Low and Medium and soft particles on Ultra only (f8c48fe); soldiers grabbed, dragged and thrown as ragdolls on a joint to the hand, impacts hurting by the speed squared like Boneworks' Enemy_Health (thr_Impact 10, mod_Impact 0.1 on relativeVelocity.sqrMagnitude; capped at 35) for 3 s after the throw (2a67c87, 4d4eb1f); fists punch (a free hand with its grip closed; at punching speed it never grabs, 8329393), gun bashes along the whole gun every 10 cm (2a67c87). The second cause of the "shadowing": in first person the game keeps the player's body shadow-only, its head at the eyes and its arms and its guns' third-person copies (Actor.weaponImposterRenderers) in the game's animation, so with shadows on that shadow lay across the hands and gun and moved on its own; now hidden outright on foot (forceRenderingOff; the player's animator never culls, so the hitboxes keep their pose), new setting BodyShadow, off (d6ce95e; on first-person seats and ladders too, f30a426). Measuring: once a minute of play the log says what share of frames kept the headset's rate (SteamVR's display frequency: Unity's read 0 Hz through Link), median and 95th frame time and the settings in use (edc08ed, 419d8cc): the user's next session says which preset holds the rate. General: key names by array and the menu's canvas hits kept sorted (no hashing per key poll, no comparer per frame, 6326e53, from a Haiku allocation scout: reports/haiku-findings-2026-10-24.txt), a magazine made on each reload no longer builds its once-only log line (0cd428d), gun bashes reach the stock's measured butt (d90dc88). The trembling hand, the lying body and the shots were calm. Kept (variant D, two runs each against the old hold and a locked hold with the same extras): the bones moved with the hand at the grab (a running soldier is ragdolled with his speed in every bone; taken running 4.3 and 3.2 m/s), the hold's turn by a damped slerp drive capped per kilogram (30 N m/rad, 3 N m s/rad, 20 N m; wrist turn 12.3 and 7.5 m/s, 12 and 11 cm), a hand turned over 20 degrees in a frame carries the body as a jump does, and the gun's stand-in ignores the body from the grab. 342. [ ] From the user (2026-10-25, after 1.12.3): "when i run the bodys still turn crazy but dont fling into the air anymore / same when i turn or walk", "theres a grab delay between my hand and the body and my hand grabs the air many times", "Punching enemys also doesnt work", "slamming them against the ground or a random object they dont take damage", and "the enemys/friendlys dont have collisions with my gun or hand" (anything in the other hand goes through a held soldier). Found with a physics-step trace in the soldier check's new walks (step 10: at the chest walking 1.5 s forward and back, running, smooth turning; `vrshots.py --grabcheck --walkcheck` runs only those): the hand's grab anchor was kinematic, and PhysX placed it right but solved its joint as if it stood still, so the hold braked the body against the world every step it was dragged. With the lock the held bone kept about 0.2 m/s while the hand went 1-3 m/s, projection dragged it along, and the solver blew up (the hold at 10^17 N, the hips at up to millions of deg/s: the spinning, the long bodies, the flinging); a soft capped drive instead lagged until the stretch let go (the user saw the test player drop the soldier walking; turning, slow, held). Fixed: the anchor is a 10 t dynamic body set going each step at the speed that takes it to the hand (walking 4-5 m/s fastest bone, the body's turn 40-100 deg/s, joints whole, in every run; the kinematic anchor in the same runs still let go). Also found: a snap turn's carry moved nested bones once per parent (the head three times, 1.5 m off the hand): every pose is computed first now. Kept from this round, each A/B'd on the fixed anchor: the player's speed changes given to every bone (fastest bone 4-5 against 6.5-7 m/s), the hold at the bone's centre of mass, 16/8 solver passes, the 30 rad/s spin cap and 5 cm limb projection while armed (no measurable cost, kept as safety). Dropped: a slippery material while held (no gain). Grabs: reach 18 cm, a 0.4 s window, and only a squeeze begun near a soldier takes hold, so a squeezed fist punches (the soldier check's new squeezed-fist punch hits and does not take hold). Slams: damage from 3.2 m/s (was 5). Still open: hands and the gun have no collision with soldiers (a design: the hands have no colliders, the gun's stand-in ignores bodies: done in 343); the hand-to-body delay; check all of it in the headset. The user's play: "The flinging etc is fixed!!". Released in 1.13.0.
