- [-] 251.28 [refuted, minor, lead] src/Weapons/GunVolume.cs:431: Hand volume is cut at the envelope faces while the stand-in keeps the same parts whole.
  What: Build clamps the measurement box to the envelope: `box.SetMinMax(Vector3.Max(box.min, envMin), Vector3.Min(box.max, envMax));` (line 431), then expands it by about 9 mm (line 436). The depth views cover only that box, so geometry beyond a face is never measured, and Solid and InsideModel return false there. GunCollider.Fit keeps any piece whose bounds merely overlap the envelope (GunCollider.cs:541-546), so the stand-in has the part and the hand does not. With the envelope in GunCollider.cs:509-510, the cut falls about 17 cm above the grip origin for non-pistols and about 8 cm behind it for pistols. Two related paths: when no renderer centre passes the margin test, Fit falls back to every renderer (GunCollider.cs:535), which brings back the far-off-mesh slice stretch that commit f01acd8 removed. And when every solid mesh lies beyond one face, the box gets negative size and Build returns null silently (GunVolume.cs:403-404 and 432-435). HeldWeapon then latches that null for the whole hold (HeldWeapon.cs:1266-1274).
  Skeptic (not real): The hand clamp (GunVolume.cs:431) and the overlap keep (GunCollider.cs:542-546) are real. But ChooseShape (HeldWeapon.cs:1449-1475) compares the mesh stand-in with the hull fitted to the same clipped volume (GunVolume.cs:617-620). MatchShape counts beyond-box interior as excess (GunCollider.cs:456, GunVolume.cs:250), so for the stated overhang the cut hull usually wins and hands and props agree. The mismatch survives only where mesh pieces win. The null latch (HeldWeapon.cs:1280-1286) and the fallback (GunCollider.cs:536) are real but rare. Better fix: Do not frame this as hand-vs-prop mismatch: for large overhangs the physics stand-in is cut at the same faces via the hull path. Real fixes: (1) if the renderer filter is added to GunVolume.For, keep the fallback to all renderers, since an empty list makes Build return null (GunVolume.cs:403). (2) Log the null once and retry it in HeldWeapon.HandSolid instead of latching _volumeTried for the hold (HeldWeapon.cs:1280-1286); a latched null makes ReloadInteractions wait out its timeout (ReloadInteractions.cs:1961). (3) If an overhang must be solid for both hands and props, drop the clamp at GunVolume.cs:431 together with the filter, so volume and hull agree.
  Skeptic (not real): The clamp at GunVolume.cs:431 hides overhang from Solid, and Fit keeps overlapping pieces (GunCollider.cs:541-546). But ChooseShape (HeldWeapon.cs:1449-1470, run in play via HandSolid) scores the mesh fit on the same clipped volume. Overhang counts as excess, so a launcher top past the face likely swaps in the clipped hull, which makes hand and stand-in agree but both lack the overhang. The stated mismatch survives only for small overhangs. The null latch is real but needs all meshes past one face. Better fix: Remove the face clamp at GunVolume.cs:431 and build from the renderers Fit uses (centre test, GunCollider.cs:528-534). The volume then covers the overhang, Match stops charging it as excess, and the mesh fit stays, so hand and stand-in agree and both keep the top of a launcher. Log once when Build returns null (GunVolume.cs:432-435). The all-renderers fallback at GunCollider.cs:536 is a separate case that needs every renderer off-envelope.
- [-] 251.29 [refuted, minor, lead] src/Core/AutoTest.cs:3066: First-shot lens flash check reads the flash before a deferred shot is decided.
  What: LensFlash.BeforeRender defers a shot whose particles are not yet counted (src/Weapons/LensFlash.cs:128, 'later'), so TryFlash and Shown++ run a frame later. The wait at lines 3060-3065 runs only while LensFlash.Instance.Shown > flashesBefore, so for a deferred shot it never waits. flashed = Shown > flashesBefore (3066) is then false, the picture is skipped, and 0 is recorded (3075).
  Skeptic (not real): The mechanism is real: LensFlash.cs:131-137 defers a same-frame shot to the next frame, and AutoTest.cs:3060 only waits while Shown moved, so a deferred first shot reads "none" at 3066. The trigger is unconfirmed: it needs the coroutine's fire to precede particle simulation, which the code cannot show. Saved run reports/shots/weapons-1016c/autotest.txt has 253 first-shot flashes shown vs 37 none, 31 of the none on guns without particles, so deferral is not systematic. Better fix: `
  Skeptic (not real): The deferral needs the first shot's particle count to be unchanged at its own frame's BeforeRender. AutoTest.cs:3038 fires the gun inside a coroutine resumed after Update, and Weapon.cs:1580-1585 plays the muzzle particles right after onSpawnProjectiles. Particles are simulated after that resume (Unity player-loop order, not checked in the repo; WeaponPatches.cs:123 and LensFlash.cs:112 are consistent with it), so a burst muzzle has its count rise before BeforeRender (LensFlash.cs:128-131). Deferral would need a muzzle system that emits nothing on its first step, which the code does not show. Impact is autotest-only. Better fix: No code change needed. If the autotest ever prints "lens flash none" for a gun whose muzzle particles play, first confirm whether LensFlash deferred that shot (log its pending state at AutoTest.cs:3066) before changing the wait at 3060.
- [-] 251.30 [refuted, tiny, lead] src/Weapons/ReloadInteractions.cs:1967: RocketFilm grabs the hand and takes a rocket before its try block.

Lines 1968-2076 (RocketModel at 1985, rocket.HoldPose at 1991 and 2001, MagazineProp.TubeFront at 2021 and 2075) contain no yield, so only a throw there matters. A throw then leaves SimulatedGrab true and the rocket prop in off.Held.
  Skeptic (not real): The pre-try hazard is structurally real: SimulatedGrab is set at ReloadInteractions.cs:1967, the only try/finally starts at 2077, and the finally is at 2149-2153. But the named trigger cannot fire. RocketModel (2294-2346) null-guards everything it touches. HoldPose (MagazineProp.cs:1244-1279) only calls static PalmSurfaceFromGrip, and HandShape.Current never returns null. TubeFront (MagazineProp.cs:1097-1130) guards GunBody. The code is autotest-only: RocketFilm is reached only from AutoTest.cs:643, 2895 and 927, behind -rfvr flags. Better fix: The proposed fix is incomplete, though the finding is not real. If the try is widened, the post-try cleanup at lines 2154-2158 (ReturnSpare(left.Rounds), then Destroy) must move into the finally. Otherwise an exception path loses the round taken by TakeFromPouch (ReloadInteractions.cs:987), because the code after the try never runs. The finally should also clear VRHand.Scripted and SimulatedGrab, as it already does.
  Skeptic (not real): The gap exists: SimulatedGrab is set at ReloadInteractions.cs:1967 and the try starts at 2077. But no trigger is reachable. HoldPose, TubeFront and TakeFromPouch also run in normal play, RocketModel is null-guarded, and MagazineParts is a non-null array (WeaponRig.cs:324). RocketFilm is autotest-only (AutoTest.cs:90 gate). The "rest of session" impact is refuted: Safely (AutoTest.cs:242-248) catches any throw, writes the report and calls Application.Quit(). Better fix: None needed. If tidied anyway: keep line 1967 as is and start the try at 1968 so the finally clears the grip. Do not Destroy the rocket in finally without returning its round: TakeFromPouch takes it from the spare (ReloadInteractions.cs:987), and the post-try block (2154-2159) already calls Ammo.ReturnSpare on the normal path, so a finally that only destroys would lose the round.
- [-] 251.31 [refuted, tiny, lead] src/Weapons/WeaponAnalyzer.cs:225: Fallback weapon rig leaves MuzzlePoint at zero, so the envelope's muzzle reach is the grip distance.
  What: Envelope computes `muzzle = (inv * (d.MuzzlePoint - origin)).z` (GunCollider.cs:507) and sets `envMax.z = Max(muzzle, 0.1) + 0.03` (line 510). Only the analysed path assigns MuzzlePoint (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:518). Fallback (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:225-240, used from the catch at lines 119-122) never sets it, and WeaponRig.cs:225 defaults it to zero. For a rifle the grip sits at z = -0.32, so envMax.z is about 0.35 m. The barrel beyond about 36 cm is then clipped from GunVolume (GunVolume.cs:431), and pieces wholly beyond it are dropped (GunCollider.cs:542-546).
  Skeptic (not real): Fallback (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:229-232) sets GunBodyPath and MuzzlePath to PathOf(root, muzzle), so the gun-body origin is the muzzle transform. FindMuzzle never returns null (lines 266-267). MuzzlePoint=0 is therefore the exact muzzle position in gun space, not a defaulted value. The envelope reach (GunCollider.cs:508-511) then covers the barrel behind the muzzle. The only residual case is a root-as-muzzle guess, which the finding does not show clips anything. The proposed fix would place the muzzle about 0.48 m past the real tip. Better fix: No fix needed. Optionally add a comment in Fallback (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:232) stating that MuzzlePoint is zero by construction because the gun body is the muzzle transform. Do not apply the proposed grip + 0.8 m offset.
  Skeptic (not real): Not real. Fallback sets GunBodyPath = PathOf(root, muzzle) (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:231), so the gun body is the muzzle transform itself (WeaponRig.cs:278; ToWorld at :419). Zero MuzzlePoint is the muzzle origin by construction, and the envelope ends at muzzle+0.03 (GunCollider.cs:511), as in the analysed path. Only the root case (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:266-267) makes zero mean the root, which is the game's own firing origin (Weapon.cs:1560). The proposed fix would put the muzzle 0.8 m past the real one. Better fix: No fix for this. Do not apply d.MuzzlePoint = GripPoint + AimAxis * 0.8: it moves the muzzle 0.8 m into empty space and enlarges the envelope and every MuzzlePoint consumer. Optional only: in the root case a geometric muzzle host (as WeaponAnalyzer.cs:436-445 does) could be used, but that changes GunBody and is a design change, not this bug.
- [-] 251.32 [refuted, tiny, helper] src/UI/SquadPointing.cs:189: Squad order label and aim dereference a missing SquadPointUi during its fade-out.
  What: open is false when ui == null (line 168), but show stays true while _alpha fades (169-170). While Pointing is true the code still reads ui.text (line 189: Pointing ? (ui.text != null ? ui.text.text : "GO TO") ...) and calls Target(ui) at line 198, which reads ui.aimPoint (line 218).
  Skeptic (not real): Not real. SquadPointUi.instance (.ref/decompiled/Assembly-CSharp/SquadPointUi.cs:24) is set only in Awake (:166) and never nulled, so after a scene change ui is a destroyed MonoBehaviour, not C# null. Unity != null makes open false (SquadPointing.cs:168), but ui.text, ui.aimPoint and ui.aimActor (189, 218-224) are plain managed field reads that do not throw. Target's native calls are guarded by Unity null checks. No NRE path.
  Skeptic (not real): SquadPointUi.instance (.ref/decompiled/Assembly-CSharp/SquadPointUi.cs:24) is set only in Awake (:166) and never nulled; no SquadPointUi OnDestroy exists. After the UI is destroyed, ui is a non-null managed reference, so ui != null (SquadPointing.cs:168) is false by Unity's overload, the fade runs, and ui.text (:56), ui.aimPoint (:54), ui.aimActor (:52) are plain field reads that do not throw. The guard at :189 short-circuits on ui.text != null. Only a cosmetic label/arrow fallback results, no exception. Better fix: None needed. The finding conflates Unity's destroyed-object null check with C# null: the reads at lines 189 and 198 do not throw on a destroyed SquadPointUi, so the code is safe as written.

Results (2026-10-16 night):
- Lead, fixed: 251.2 (daccda3: WeaponRigData.Handgun by slot for what a gun is; IsPistol stays for how it is held); 251.3, 251.4 (TubeRoom; InsertMagazine too), 251.6, 251.7, 251.8, 251.12 (AuthoredAuto), 251.13 (with e883241: never the launcher, a rocket-sized mesh of its own), 251.14, 251.17, 251.19 (3a79286); 251.5 (e883241: the line reaches 12 cm out, no snap); flashfilm.py reads comma decimals.
- Lead, not done: 251.9 (keep-out one frame late: 11 ms, and eased at 30/s anyway); 251.10 (an override naming a magazine would have to be taken before detection so its seat and bounds follow: overrides are rarely used); 251.11 only the log and comment corrected (an edit reaches weapons analysed after it); 251.22 only the comment (an unseated magazine falls, as in play).
- Helper (sent 2026-10-16 night): 251.1, 251.15, 251.16, 251.18, 251.21. 251.20 / 251.23 not a bug: the derivative and the spring share the clamped time step, so the push equals the real change of the hand's speed.
- Still to change after the next merge (helper's files): GunCollider's envelope (509-510, 552), Casings 95, HeldWeapon recoil (900, 904) and mass (975) by Handgun instead of IsPistol: a rifle held like a pistol got a pistol-sized shape envelope.

### For 252: what the read-only Haiku scouts found (2026-10-16 night) and what was done

- (m) Seated magazine jumping to the hand: PullSeatedMagazine put it on the rail with RailLateral0 = 0, and the rail's 7 cm off-line test knocked it off on the first frame (the grab zone is ~10 cm), then it blended straight to the hand through the receiver. Fixed: the grab's lateral offset is the baseline, and a magazine still in its well stays on the rail up to 20 cm off-line (it leaves only out of the mouth).
- (j) Spare magazine behind pistols: no pistol still from weapons-1016c shows one (the stills are from the front). Likeliest cause: CollectSpareMagazines only takes spares resting 28 cm or more from the grip; a spare held just behind the pistol stays drawn. Done: 12 cm for handguns. Unsure stills to check: weapon-062-GLOCK_43X-S (dark oval left of the pistol), weapon-075-vector / 080-mwii-g17 / 106-apc45 (a slab bottom right). Other possible causes: on the bore line within 6 cm, the caps (4 spares, 8 props), skinned ARS guns (other magazine bones hidden only within 3 cm).
- (k) MK18RII light and laser backwards: nothing in the mod or the game turns them round; most likely the emitter in the asset faces back (mk18riii is in no rig dump; its .rfc is unreadable here). Done: an emitter (LaserSight or spot Light) more than 120 degrees off the bore is turned round every frame (logged once). Same family to watch: light_mk18_acog (056), light_mk18_EXPS3 (057); HK416-B's Laser Sight under HK416控制器.
- (e) M1 GARRET: the pouch's "empty clip" was the emptied clip lying by the hip: a dropped magazine within 13 cm of the palm beat the pouch, whatever its rounds (any gun). Fixed: an empty one is never taken with the palm in the pouch. Still to do (218, en-bloc mode): it locks open only if analysed SelfLoading with a slide (a Manual one never locks: OnShot locks only when CyclesItself); its clip axis is (0, -0.70, -0.72) so the clip goes in up and forward instead of down from the top (MagAxisFromShape assumes a magazine below the bore); nothing ejects the clip after the last shot and no ping cue is bound (MechSound has none; find the game's ping in its SoundBank / reload cues); the bolt should close on its own when a clip is seated.
- (g) Hands and magazines through guns, ranked: 1. the glove's keep-out was eased in (28% a frame), so a fast shove left it ~7 cm inside the gun: fixed, pushed out at once and eased back only; 2. the free hand's glove is checked a frame late and ShortestWayOut can exit on the far side (to do: sweep from its last clear place, exit against its motion); 3. a magazine 30 cm behind the hand jumped to it through the gun: fixed, it stays outside and is let go 45 cm on; 4. the magwell was open 1.5 cm a side: fixed (4 mm); 5. off the well line the magazine hand's fingers do not close on the gun (to do); 6. the freed-from-inside search can exit the far side (to do); 7. no keep-out until the solid is measured (to do: fall back to the envelope box); 8. parts outside the collider envelope (long suppressors, wide rails on handguns) have no solid (to do); 9-10. fingers starting inside, the free hand's fingers get no closure (to do).
- (h) Magazines going in through the gun on many guns (pistols, HK416-B/D, CZ Bren 2 BR): the HK416-B's 30% picture shows the magazine 2 cm back toward the trigger guard: its line is the reload animation's pull, 18 degrees toward the grip (MagAxisFromShape trusts the pull over an unreadable shape's box within 20 degrees). Done: only on handguns now; a long gun's box line is used. Also the well opening (4 mm). Next: fit each gun's magazine line to its measured solid once it is measured (candidates +-24 degrees in the side plane, the least of the magazine passing through the gun outside its well).

- (w) Night vision: it is the game's own goggles (GameManager.defaultNightVisionGoggles, toggled by the off-hand grip beside the head, BodyZones 209-214 -> KeyBinds.Goggles). Its image effect (GenericImageEffect OnRenderImage, or the NV post-process profile) runs on the flat game camera only (CameraSuppressor sets those cameras to draw to the desktop only); the eyes get only the grey actor glow and the NV canvas turned into a 72-degree HUD frame. To do: our own night-vision pass on the eye camera (OnRenderImage on VRRig.Camera while NightVision.isEnabled: luminance gain, green tint, grain, vignette), or copy the game's GenericImageEffect material; hide the game's grey vignette in VR meanwhile.
- (p) Two weapons at once: nothing exists yet beyond 152's hand swap. The mod knows one held weapon (WeaponSystem.Held) and the game one activeWeapon; taking another weapon goes through TakeIntoHand -> actor.SwitchWeapon, which holsters the first. Design (staged): a second HeldWeapon bound to the off hand (WeaponSystem.Second; HeldWeapon parameterised by its gun and support hands), shown without being the game's active weapon (the slot trick at WeaponDrops 1124), fired with the off hand's own trigger through Weapon.Shoot (semi latched, auto on the cooldown), its own AmmoState; pairs by Weapon.slot / type (primary 0, secondary 1, launcher = LargeGear 3, grenade = ThrowableWeapon; never two primaries); per-hand SuppressFire; one GunCollider per held weapon; letting go of the second never holsters the main one; reloading either while both hands are full is blocked in v1 (holster one first).
- Support hand trigger: the left trigger is the game's Use (not Fire). On a gun it now works the pump, fires an under-barrel launcher (switched to while held), or steps the scripts' laser / light keys; while the support hand holds such a gun, that trigger is not passed to the game as Use.

- (u) Shotguns reloadable: in the last full run every shotgun loaded full by hand and fired (R870 BUCKSHOT/SLUG 8/8, R870 CQC 6/6, mw-m870 8/8, M1014 and Tactical 8/8, ARS M500 and Bot Friendly 7/7; DOUBLE-BARREL, SAWED-OFF and the six M1878s 2/2). To recheck in the headset with the pump now on the support hand's trigger.
- (v) Break-action shells: Casings.Drop returned when the gun had no flat casing particles (the vanilla DOUBLE-BARREL and SAWED-OFF), after the count was already taken: fixed, an opened break action always lets its fired cases out. Still to do: the shell in the hand and the ones thrown out are the mod's 12-gauge lathe, not the gun's own model (CollectReloadRounds skips parts hidden at rest and never uses RoundName; a shell part could become MagazinePath before the hinge is found): copy the gun's shell part (a new ShellPath) for the hand shell, the dropped ones and the chamber; one "shell" rule for KindOf and ShotgunShells (a revolver's .357 is a red shell in the hand, brass when dropped).

- (n) Machine gun boxes: no belt-fed gun has an in-hand test picture (MagazineDeepCheck skips a box locked under a shut cover, and "pulled out by hand: stays in the hand" only checks a prop exists). Likeliest cause of both symptoms: a belt part wins the magazine pick (MagName has belt / ammo / 弹链, weight 3, while a box named 弹箱 scored 1): a long belt is "only the belt comes out", a link a few centimetres long is "only the number shows". Done: on a belt-fed gun (40+ rounds, automatic) a belt piece (BeltName, or a plain "ammo"/"AMMO.001" part) is never the box (under 6 cm skipped, otherwise weight 0.5); ammo box / ammo can / box mag / 弹箱 count as magazine names. To do: log the chosen magazine part and copy-or-stand-in at the first eject; let the deep check open the cover and take the box (and check something visible is in the hand); MK46 ET's analysed box is 65 cm against a 31 cm copy (belt start from the wrong height); the tall black block at mg3's feed end (weapon-153).
- (ac) The mwii M4s' charging handle cannot be cut out (their meshes are unreadable) and no shader can clip it at draw time without a shader bundle built in a Unity 2020.3 editor (none on this PC). Done: the added piece on an AR-pattern gun is a slim T latch, not a cube.

- (e) M1 GARRET en-bloc mode coded (untested): by name (garand / garret), the clip goes in from the top only with the action open, seating it sends the bolt home, the last shot throws the empty clip up with the gun's own ping (its "Bank Audio Source", garand_clip) and the bolt stays open.

### For 252 (ae): every gun's rack, checked on its pictures (20 read-only Haiku agents on weapons-1017, 2026-10-17)

18 agents compared each gun's rack close-ups at rest and pulled half way (cameras fixed to the gun), 13 guns each (234 guns); 2 more looked at the seating pictures of the guns the side test flagged. Their verdicts are per picture and often low confidence; the lines below are as they read them.

Summary (234 guns):
- OK, the right part moves with the hand: most pistols (FNX-45, P220, 1911, Glock-type, P320, 92g, S-IND7, AF2011), the HK416 family, BRN180s, mk18riii, light mk18s, ak12 squad / ak12h squad families, MP9 family, ak74m, R870s, mw-m870, apc45, SL-DEFENDER, AUTOMATICO, PATRIOT TAC, ARS MP9.
- The right part moves but the TEST's glove is beside it (HAND_OFF_PART, the gun works): PKM / PKP / Type 80 (the lever moves 65 mm), QJB201 family (the cocking rod moves ~205 px), QBZ192 / QBZU191 family (the paddle moves), M1014s, SUPERNOVA, SIGNAL DMR, JOLT-20-S, f2000s, R870 CQC, GLOCK 43X family, ARS Glock 19, PATRIOT / PATRIOT GL, MK46 ET. Fixed: the grab point goes to the piece of the slide that sticks out (paddle, knob, lever), and the PATRIOTs get their 1016c analysis back.
- WRONG part moves: M4A1 CCO / EOT family (204-209; a block cut out of the receiver: REGRESSION, fixed: the carve guard missed "carrying the bolt"); M4A1 / acog / reddot (182-184, the [SQUAD] family?) and K13 family (6), m733 x3 (the bolt inside moves, the T handle stays: still open); mwii-m4 x3 (the added block: known, 252 ac); BUNDLE O' BUSS (the far barrel moves).
- NOTHING visibly moves: [Sec] BRN180 x3 (fixed: a long two-handed gun in the secondary slot is a rifle), Vector x2 and mw-vector x2, QUICKSILVER, INGRESS 700, PPSh-41, ARS M500 (both), 76 EAGLE, asvalokp7; .357 CONDOR is a revolver.

Checked again after the fixes (2026-10-17, targeted runs check-1017b on 8 guns, check-1017c on 8 guns):
- check-1017b: the pump worked by the support hand's trigger works (held by the pump, the grip alone moves nothing, the trigger takes it, a full stroke cycles a round, let go: back on the pump); the empty reload with a real hand rack passes on the GLOCK 43X, M4A1, CZ Bren 2 BR, QJB201 Mag and K13 ACOG; an empty magazine at the pouch is passed over for a full one; a magazine taken 7 cm off the well's line stays on its rail; the en-bloc cycle passes (clip refused with the bolt shut, in through the open action, the bolt home on its own, 8 shots, the clip thrown with the gun's own ping, the bolt locked open); a grenade in the free hand: pin pulled by its trigger, thrown live; the hands' shape now costs at most 1.5 ms in any one frame (was 11.5 ms on average, up to 17, in one frame), a magazine's 1.5 ms (was 7). It also showed: the cover check never took the cover (the grip was only held, never pressed: fixed), belt-fed guns failed to fire after the empty-reload check (their belt came back unlaid: they skip it now), en-bloc steps offered a clip with the bolt shut (fixed).
- check-1017c: problems 18 -> 8. M1 GARRET chambers and fires 3/3; PATRIOT back to its bolt + its own 'Charging Handle' (2/4 fingers on it); PKM: the cover is taken at its latch, opens, falls open, shuts, and it fires 3/3; QJB201: the same, and its hand is now taken to 'QJB201_Handle'; the [Sec] BRN180 finds its charging handle like its [Gear] twin; the M4A1 CCO's receiver is no longer cut (3-4 of 4 fingers on the part).
- The user's screenshot during check-1017c (a gun standing up at the right edge, a dark block above it): the holster display of the [Sec] BRN180, left in the secondary slot by the test: a long gun in the hip holster was centred on the hip, so an 80 cm rifle stood past the shoulder. Not only the test: any long gun in the secondary slot. Fixed: it hangs lower, its top at most 20 cm above the hip.
- M4A1 [SQUAD] (prefab M4A1squad: 182-184, user's 252 a), fixed and checked in check-1017d: the model's reload animation moves only its bolt carrier (对象004), never its T; the carrier had been taken for "its own handle" and slid in the port while the T stood still. Now the small part at the carrier's rear top that the animations leave still is the T handle (对象006 / 对象001_002 / 对象002_001): "the charging handle" moves with the hand (1-4 of 4 fingers on it), the carrier follows, the empty reload still passes. The CCO / EOT family (204-209) is unchanged by it.
- Rig dump (reports/shots/dump-1017/rig-dump.txt): m733s take their named handle 'mk18_拉机柄' and 4 drawn parts move with it (the review's "T on the right" may have been the forward assist: unclear, not wrong); K13 family: '416F QJ' (the bolt, fire 10.5 cm) is the slide, the T is not found ('416F ZG' 5.4 cm static under '416F BOLT' is a candidate; K13 is not in the AR name list); Vector: no part moves in any animation, its 'slide' (taken by name) sits inside, and the visible cocking knob is a separate part 'weapon_tdi_kriss_vector_slide_rotation_9x19_LOD0_0' that is not its child: parts named after the slide should move with it (to do).

Their prefabs have no enabled first-person renderers (the autotest's model check counts them as "invisible by design", like zombie claws); they carry what bots need. Option: draw the weapon's third-person model (WeaponDrops.ModelFor) in the hand when the first-person one has nothing to draw.

#### For 252 (ae): the reviewers' plans for the open guns, and the measuring run (2026-10-17 afternoon)

Three Haiku workflows (a scout and 2 skeptics per item, read only) looked at K13, Vector, the 1911 10R line, the keep-out, the rack hand pose, QUICKSILVER, INGRESS 700, 76 EAGLE, PPSh-41, BUNDLE O' BUSS, xm8, ARS MP9 (magazine drawn when out, a part 44 mm back after the release) and RECON LRR (rounds lost on a slow mag-kick push). Every verdict was "partly": the mechanisms mostly hold, but every skeptic said to MEASURE FIRST (the rig dump had no positions, nothing was run). Their full texts: scratchpad digests wf1/wf2/wf3-summary.md of this session (not in the repo); the plans per item:
- K13: a T at the carrier's rear top that the animations leave still (as M4A1 [SQUAD]); check '416F ZG' by position first (same parent as the carrier, static, 3-12 cm, rear top), gate on SlideMethod "fire motion" and no handle.
- Vector: "slide companions": static parts named like the slide (word match, no 'lod' or numbered copies), touching the slide's box (+3 cm), at most the slide's size, moved with the slide's travel and put back on release. The knob's travel must be capped to its slot (about 2 cm by the picture: measure), the grab point moved to the knob later. Do NOT use an AABB "inside the receiver" test (the AK and PPSh gun bodies are whole-gun meshes: false alarms).
- 1911 10R / FA1911 10R / FAQ1911 / Q1911: the vertex axis leans 13-16 degrees; two candidates: parked sibling meshes whose GameObject is active but renderer off feed the covariance (ActiveUpTo tests activeSelf only, not MeshRenderer.enabled), or the top tangent over a 4 cm baseline. Controls D1911 (+0.06) and FA1911 20R (+0.40). Do not touch FitMagazineLine for it.
- Keep-out: the well's opening is exempted per point, whether or not the magazine is on its line; or the 25 points straddle a plate between their rows (4-5.5 cm apart); or the gate (0.7 m from the grip). Probe first.
- Rack hand pose: GLOCK 43X takes a handle on the slide's front (a handle search with no handgun guard); SUPERNOVA / JOLT / QBZ192 points snapped onto the receiver, not onto their knob or paddle; PKM lever below the bore gets a palm over the top (RackHandPose's 2.5 cm sideways rule, test only); M1014 the same; f2000's lever point not on a surface; R870 CQC analysed as a charging handle instead of a pump.
- QUICKSILVER: 'Charging Handle.001' (fire 11.4 cm) is the slide; whether it is hidden must be measured before a knob is added (not by the "out of the receiver" log line, GREASER has it too).
- INGRESS 700: the 'Charge port' (4 cm) beats the visible 'Slide_high' since its bolt cap became 23 cm (the cap follows Handgun, slot 1 only); fix candidates: the cap by IsPistol, or a length factor in the fire-motion score; needs a before/after diff of every gun's slide.
- 76 EAGLE: the pump block moves only ~15 mm of a 51 mm pull: find where the pull is lost (UpdateRack vs ApplyPartOverrides) first.
- PPSh-41: the grab piece is 'Safety'; the bolt never moves rearward from rest in its animations (open bolt): settle the stroke before adding a knob.
- BUNDLE O' BUSS: name what moves (a carve piece, a bone or an animated transform) before changing the carve; a knob instead is a product choice.
- xm8: RackOnOutstandingPiece took a 0.0 cm '拉机柄' sibling's box; use the piece's own mesh box and its outer face, and do not snap a piece's point onto the whole gun.
- ARS MP9 magazine (232): find which bone draws the leftover (MagSwap, Body 1 or none) by vertex weights before any rule change.
- ARS MP9 44 mm: name the part off home and the animator's pose (empty or loaded) after the release before changing anything.
- RECON LRR 29 -> 15: the fresh magazine left the hand during the slow push by an unknown branch; logged now.

### For 256: the sandbox (2026-10-17 evening)

- tools/sandbox/app (PyQt6 + PyQt6-Fluent-Widgets, own OpenGL 3.3 view; numpy, Pillow); exe by `python tools/sandbox/build_exe.py` into tools/sandbox/bin/RavenfieldVR-Sandbox/ (229 MB: tensorflow / torch etc. excluded, they made it 1.7 GB). The exe finds the repo upward from itself.
- Data: one export run of the game (`python tools/sandbox/export.py [--weapons ...]`, or Advanced → Export weapons): `-novr -nointro -force-gfx-direct -rfvr-selftest -rfvr-export`; per weapon model.glb (renderers at rest in gun space, right-handed; skinned meshes posed at idle; textures; spare magazines, reload props and parts parked > 90 cm from the grip left out) and rig.json (the analysis). Into reports/sandbox/data/weapons (ignored by git). About 50 s for a few guns.
- Unreadable meshes (many mod packs: GLOCK 43X, Vector, M1 GARRET...) are read back from the graphics card (GpuMeshReader: D3D11 staging copy of the native vertex / index buffers, decoded with the mesh's vertex layout; export run only, single-threaded rendering). Worth trying in the mod itself for 252 (ac): readable copies of unreadable meshes would let the bolt piece be cut out of them.
- The glove is the mod's (glove.bin, the same posing math and 31 poses); the grip hand sits exactly as the mod holds a gun (GunFrame = the glove's palm frame at the grip point, Quest's automatic gun angle). In VR the glove then settles onto the grip surface (up to 2 cm), the sandbox shows the raw place.
- Found with it: the M1 GARRET's clip seat was its parked place 61 cm off the bore (the reload parks the clip again at its end): now the seat is where the reload brings the clip nearest the bore (in the receiver), for en-bloc guns by name too.
- Saved poses: tools/sandbox/poses/<weapon>.json and BepInEx/config/RavenfieldVR/poses/<weapon>.json (both hands in the mod's gun space, curls, the part each holds, each hand's job). The mod reads them (SandboxPoses, config Weapons.UseSandboxPoses, default on): the gun hand's place and turn become the grip point and a grip frame (HeldWeapon and the second weapon hold the gun by it), its curls the weapon's own grip curls; a left hand whose job is the handguard sets the support point and curls. Checked: a pose saved right after "Hands on the gun" gives the mod's own grip back (0.006 mm, 1e-5), so only a changed pose changes anything (to try in the headset).
- Open: a settle of the glove onto the grip surface in the sandbox; the weapon's skinned bones (ARS family) do not move in the sandbox (the slide / handle of skinned guns is a bone); textures of custom shaders may come out white (only _MainTex is read).

- Save and share (user, 2026-10-17: "make sure our app works and we can save and import things"): Save pose (here and for the game), Import pose..., Export pose..., Send to game / Remove from game, and a line saying which one the game uses. `main.py --selftest report.txt [--weapon NAME]` (the exe too) runs every feature as a user would: 21 checks, pose files it touches put back. 21/21 on GLOCK 43X, M4A1 CCO, mg3, DOUBLE-BARREL, vector (python) and PKM, GLOCK 43X, vector (exe), exit 0.
- Exit: a normal Python exit crashed now and then in teardown (access violation, about 1 run in 8): the GPU resources are freed on close and the process ends with os._exit. That first version flushed stdout, which is None in the exe (no console): "AttributeError: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'flush'" on every close of the exe (the user saw it, 2026-10-17 ~20:43); fixed the same evening, exe closes with 0 (3/3).
- Review (6 Haiku reviewers, read-only, 2026-10-17 evening; each finding checked against the code before a fix). Fixed: the saved grip was the SETTLED place (a pose saved without changes moved the game's grip 1.3-2.7 cm: settle now moves only the drawn glove, as HandModel does); rig.json was written before the weapon's overrides (now after: the sandbox holds an overridden gun as the game does); two hands took the gun's up from d.Up, not the palm (a rolled saved palm rolled the gun when the handguard was taken); recoil in the palm frame (now the bore's); the thumb never reached the game (now thumbSet: only a thumb posed in the sandbox, else the mod's thumb rule); a dragged left hand kept a stale job and was dropped from the saved pose; the fresh magazine was not drawn while going in; the cover snap and latch ignored the cover's openness (and seatOnHinge, now exported); textures were never freed; a new weapon kept the last laser pick; plus file names, failed saves, job start errors, GIF timing and memory, the pose list, the pointing pose (Valve's joints). Refuted: the palm skin "7 mm too close" (the mod grows the gun by Grow and pulls the skin in by the same: the same contact as the raw skin on the raw mesh); export folder clashes (286 folders for 286 weapons; a warning added anyway).
- Open: re-export so rig.json carries seatOnHinge and the overrides; verify in the game that a saved pose is read (log "hand pose from the sandbox") and holds the gun where the sandbox showed; the ARS MP9's skinned-part split to look at in the sandbox.

- Recheck of the 110 confirmed findings (real2.txt) against the code of 2026-10-18 afternoon (10 read-only Haiku agents, each with code evidence): 64 fixed, 1 obsolete, 43 'partly' (the bug fixed, an extra part of the suggestion not built), 2 not fixed. Fixed the same day after it: the warnings line read a letter at a time (B9#4), an export's refresh kept the hands of a reload still playing (B2#2), Copy coordinates on a parked magazine gave its parked place 1 m off on the M1 (B2#3, now its seat), a free drag ran off the pointer in the orthographic view (B4#4), a failed or cut-off export left a gun with no export or copied nothing (I10#6, B7#1: copied beside and swapped in, and copied also when interrupted), GIF colours shimmered (one palette from the first frame), errors shown were not in the error log (I10#4), and the rack hand on a side handle hung upside down (I6#3: the two thumb signs were swapped; fingers now up over it as the game holds it, checked on the AS Val, with a self-test check). Still not built (extras of the suggestions): 4x / transparent screenshots, a .txt beside each picture, a preview pane in Captures, a '?' key and a cursor change, arrow-key orbit, F on the selected hand, a from-muzzle switch for the numbers, the thumb of the gun's own grip in the preview, an empty-gun toggle for the reload, a 'copy pose as text', the game folder from Steam's library list, more self-test steps (export, game test), sandbox-and-game role rules (a left hand behind the grip saved as support, a right hand on the magazine saved as grip).
- Found with the sandbox export (2026-10-17 late, numeric sweep over all 288 guns + pictures): (1) a muzzle far past the drawn barrel on 43 guns (every FNX-45 about 1 m, PPSh-41 94 cm, M1878 Sawed-Offs 37 cm, RPG-7s, PKM Z suppressor variants): the mod's length, swing, support-hand slide limit, collider envelope, reports and the pistol-or-rifle rule all used it; now taken at the drawn barrel's end (only the parts that hang together with the gun body count; the game still fires from its own muzzle). (2) Every pistol was "not a handgun" in the match: the menu warm-up analyses prefabs, whose Weapon.slot is -1, and the match used that cached result; the slot now comes from the weapon entry (62 handguns now, 0 before). (3) Under-barrel grenade launchers modelled barrel-up (HK416-D M320, QBZ192-D QLG, PATRIOT GL) had up = (0,0,0); now from the weapon they hang under. (4) The sandbox crashed on them, and any error in a button ended the program: errors are now logged to reports/sandbox/sandbox-errors.log and shown, the program goes on.
- Still flagged by the sweep, to judge gun by gun: parts drawn away from the gun at rest (mwii-g17 family's receiver piece 53 cm away, FNX-45 RDS "Sight (1)" 27 cm in front, PKM Z suppressor parked behind, shotgun shells / grenade shells beside the gun, M417A2 'Mag.001' 45 cm away); rack points off any moving part (92g family 6.6 cm, AK-24 12 cm, CZ Bren 2 BR family 5.5 cm, M417A2 17 cm, Suzuya AR-15s 16 cm, asval 11 cm); magazine seat off the magazine (PKM / PKP / Type 80 family 9.9 cm, MK46 ET 19.9 cm, G BRN180 EO SS 11.5 cm); grip in the air (HK416-D 6.4 cm, QBZ192-D 8.5 cm, Panzerfaust 3 15 cm).
- 20 read-only Haiku agents on the sandbox program (10 bug hunters, 10 improvement scouts, each finding checked by a skeptic), user's request 2026-10-17 late: 142 findings, 110 confirmed, 32 refuted. Batch 1 (commit a6594b7, data safety and correctness) and batch 2 part 1 (ddf1662) done by me; the rest split between 7 Haiku 5.5 fix agents, one git worktree each (rvr-wt-V/M/C/G/H/D/T, branches sandbox-fix-*), merged and tested by me. Left as an approximation: the thumb of the weapon's own grip in the preview (the game solves it to contact with the grip; I6#6).
- Sandbox review, final state (2026-10-17 23:50): all 110 confirmed findings handled. Done by me: batch 1 (a6594b7) and batch 2 part 1 (ddf1662); by 7 Haiku 5.5 fix agents in their own worktrees, merged and tested by me (7f02229): V viewport (Ctrl/Ctrl+Shift drag moves/turns the weapon, zoom/pan scale, cached uniforms, role at drag end, debounced weapon list, early window, axis key, pick normal), M menu (hand on any picked part, Copy the hands for Claude, Reset the weapon's place, F frames gun + hands, keypad views, tooltips), C Captures page (thumbnails, copy, delete to the Recycle Bin), G pictures (clean, 2x, turntable GIF, Record the reload), H selected-hand card (touches, settle, saved diff, Revert, typed angles kept), D exe (version resource, taskbar id, 229 -> 216 MB), Help, README, T self-test (controls, keys, snaps, sliders; SKIP lines). Fixed with the mod's own JSON reader (Core/MiniJson.cs, commit ddf1662); check-1017j (M4A1 CCO alone) reads it exactly: grip (0.02553, 0.18396, -0.12432), the rolled palm, curls, thumb base 0.7 / curl 0.55, the support point. ### For 257: floating spare ammunition (2026-10-18 night)

- mwii-g17 family: its spare 'mag_001' waits 58 cm from the grip but only 6 cm off the bore's line, and the spare rule took
  anything near the bore's line as a suppressor; the gate is now only for what lies ahead of the grip. Hidden while held.
- New rule ParkedAmmo (after the reload rounds, every part chosen by then): every drawn part grouped with what it touches
  within 2 cm; a group is parked ammunition when it is not the gun at the grip, holds a part named as ammunition (mag, 弹,
  round words, gauge/loader/follower) or of the magazine's size, and nothing in it is the gun's (a chosen part, a mesh over
  60 cm, anything on the bore's line ahead of the grip, anything where the seated magazine is, a sight, a key / button /
  release / handle / 把 / 柄 / "in_gun" / chamber). Not for two-gun weapons (akimbo, dual, double, bundle, several muzzles).
- Hidden now (44 guns): BRN180 family (14) 'xmaglrg_shell' + 1 = an X-Mag drum 13 cm left of the stock and 22 cm under it
  (beside the player's chest, never in the pictures); FNX-45 family (12) 'Magazine Tac' + 3 parts, 11-23 cm; HK416 family
  (10) the spare 'HK416弹匣.001' + its round '子弹.001' behind the stock; M417A2 'Mag.001' 45 cm; QJB201 family (6) a belt
  of 11 rounds behind the stock (3 cm from its butt).
- Wrong catches found on the way and ruled out: K13 '416F MAG KEY' (its magazine release), AT-1K '提把' (carrying handle),
  M1014 Tactical '12_guage_in_gun', the AR-15 GL's launcher grenade (loaded, 4 cm under the bore; looked 22 cm off while the
  rifle's one-piece 98 cm body was left out of the gun). A first version measured each part against the gun's small parts
  only; a big mesh's box then either left loaded rounds looking apart (AR-15 GL) or, counted as the gun, swallowed spares
  (the HK416's undrawn 'Cube' ended 3.3 cm from its spare): groups judge both right.
- Left drawn on purpose: akimbo pairs' second gun; PKM / PKP / Type 80 belt links (drawn by the belt, checked in the game);
  the PATRIOT launcher's loaded grenade.
- Open: shotguns whose chosen "magazine" is a shell parked off the gun at rest (76 EAGLE 'Shell' 11 cm, M1014
  '12_guage_loader' 7 cm, R870 '12_guage' 20 cm): the magazine's seat is taken from where it rests, so a seated shell may be
  drawn there; ARS Glock 19 'Glock_ThirdPerson' 19 cm off; FNX-45 [RDS] 'Sight (1)', a 16 cm square plane 27 cm ahead.
  Check-1018a (VR, the new side pictures) is for these.
- Checked in the game (2026-10-18 morning, VR null driver, the autotest's new weapon-side-NNN picture: the held gun from
  its side, 1.3 m off): check-1018a (HK416, HK416-A, M417A2, FNX-45, BRN180 L, R870, M1014, 76 EAGLE, ARS Glock 19) and
  check-1018b (mwii-g17, PATRIOT GL, 9 QJB201 variants): no spare magazine, drum, belt or round beside or behind any of
  them; the mwii-g17's spare (the user's) is gone; the shotguns' chosen shells and the Glock's third-person mesh do not
  show either; the PATRIOT GL's grenade stays in its launcher. (What shows in those pictures besides the gun: the
  player's holstered loadout and the magazines the test dropped.)

### For 260: the mag kick's flight, the magazine hitbox, a magazine against the seated one (2026-10-18)

- (a) Kick flight: `ReloadInteractions.KickOut` gave the knocked-out magazine a sideways push (1.3 m/s) and a downward one
  with no forward part, and spun it about the barrel axis (a roll), so it dropped straight down. Now it flies forward
  along the barrel (1.4 to 1.9 m/s, with the strike's push and the gun hand's motion), 0.6 m/s away from the gun hand and
  0.2 m/s down; its spin is 6 to 9 rad/s about insert x aim, which carries the top forward (end over end, top leading).
  The release button's drop (`FinishEject`) is unchanged: it still drops straight out.
- (b) Hitbox: `ShapeFit.Thin` kept every n-th outline point of a piece once the piece had more than 180 (about ten slices of
  1.5 cm: most rifle and long pistol magazines), and the stride skipped the outermost points, so the piece's hull stopped
  short of the model. Autotest before (check-1018a, thrown magazine): 43% (ARS Glock 19), 76% (BRN180 L), 53% (FNX-45) of
  the model covered. Now it keeps, for 180 directions spread over the sphere, the point farthest along each direction, so
  the hull is the hull of all the outline points (the same for guns and the unreadable-mesh fit). Not changed: a curved
  magazine's pieces still bulge (BRN180 L: 67% outside), because magazines are capped at 8 pieces (coarser slices).
- (c) Magazine against the seated one: `MagazineProp.KeepOffGun` let the magazine in the hand pass any point inside the
  well box (the well's opening, 8 mm play) while it was on the well line, and the seated magazine is inside that box, so
  the fresh one went through it (the autotest's keep-out "sideways slowly" had 7 frames inside the gun). The well is open
  to the gun's body only now: `HeldWeapon.SeatedMagSolid` (the seated magazine as posed, grown by a finger's radius, the
  same as in `HandsSolid`) blocks it. The fresh magazine stops at the seated one's base; a strike or a firm push still
  kicks the seated one out, because `TryMagKick` reads the hand's pose, not the drawn one. The autotest's counts
  (`MagazineProp.PointsInsideGun`, `ProbeInside`) use the same rule, so a magazine pushed into the seated one shows.
- Not run here (no build and no game in this session). Check in the headset: the kicked magazine flies forward and turns
  end over end; a fresh magazine stops at the seated base and kicks out with a strike and with a push. Autotest: the
  thrown magazine's shape coverage should be above the 43 to 76% above, and "magazine keep-out (sideways slowly)" 0 frames.

### For 263: performance (2026-10-18 evening, wf_e1a3995c)

- What the measurements say (check-1018h, a clean 2-gun VR run; weapons-1017, all 290 guns): the mod's steady cost is
  small (all sections together about 0.5 ms a frame; GPU 4.5 ms). The lag is spikes: single frames of Weapons.BeforeRender
  up to 92-449 ms and Weapons.Update up to 136 ms, an enemy's weapon drop 10-29 ms and 0.1-1.1 MB of garbage each, frames
  99th 18.3 ms, max 384 ms, 110 late and 160 dropped in about 2 minutes.
- 8 read-only Haiku hunters (first use, drops, BeforeRender, update loops, garbage, GPU, physics, long sessions) reported 63
  candidates; a Haiku skeptic each kept 7 (all one-off spikes, no per-frame cost worth changing). 4 Haiku fixers by file
  (a worktree each) and a verifier each; merged by me, built clean; the GunVolume fixer's asynchronous measurement had no
  caller (another fixer owned HeldWeapon): wired in by me.
- Changed:
  - The first hold of a gun whose slide is found by its fire motion measured how much of the slide shows (12 pictures, each
    read back from the GPU at once: 12 stalls and a few MB of garbage in that frame; P320, 1911, ACE 32 in the user's log).
    Now GunVolume.VisibleShareAsync reads them back as the GPU finishes (a frame or two later) and the knob, if any, is added
    then (HeldWeapon.KnobOnHiddenSlide / AddKnobOnHiddenSlide; once per type; nothing added after the gun is put away). The
    rack self-test keeps the synchronous one.
  - The first hold of each gun type cooked all its hull pieces (Physics.BakeMesh) on the main thread in one frame and
    rebuilt the physics stand-in up to three times: the cooks are now spread over frames (about 1.5 ms a frame) and the
    shape is chosen once they are done (HeldWeapon).
  - BoltPiece.Carve (the handle cut out of a receiver on the first draw of a gun not in the loadout): each corner measured
    once, the centroid test only where a triangle's corners all pass, fewer whole-mesh copies; same output.
  - WeaponWarmup prepares the drop templates and their shapes for more weapon types at the menu, so an enemy's death does not
    build a cold type's drop in that frame.
- Not changed (judged not safe without a run): the first-hold shape comparisons (MatchShape), the magazine's first-hold
  hull cook (MagazineProp.TakeRenderedShape), ScaledPieces cooking per holder scale on a death frame.
- To measure: the autotest's Performance block (Weapons.BeforeRender and Weapons.Update max, 'weapon drop' per call,
  frames 99th / max, late and dropped) on check-1018h's guns and a full weapons run, against the numbers above.

### For 264-270: the user's headset test of 2026-10-18 evening, 7 Haiku fixers (wf_918b456f)

- 7 Haiku fixers (a git worktree each, nothing built or run) with a verifier each; merged by me in order, one conflict
  (SecondWeapon.BeforeRender: both the arms and the spares hidden), built clean (2026-10-18 ~19:00). Not yet run in the
  game: the user asked to pause after this. Evidence used: the user's own session log, reports/shots/user-1018-evening/
  LogOutput.log (copied before the next run overwrites it).
- 264 (slide forward by itself): ReleaseSlide / EndRack / the trigger release called AmmoState.RackForward, which unlocked
  the slide and chambered only with rounds in; the only re-catch (EndRack) needed a magazine in with 0 rounds. So a slide
  released with the magazine out stayed free and unchambered, and a loaded magazine then made HeldWeapon.LeaveEmptyPose
  cross-fade the animator to its loaded rest pose (the user's log: "Magazine ejected from NVO-228E#ACE 32: 0 rounds", then
  "the animator stayed in its empty pose with a round loaded"). Now (AmmoState.RackForward and AdoptWeaponAmmo): with
  nothing chambered, a lock-on-empty gun that is not cycled by hand and uses a chamber stays caught back; the trigger
  releases the slide only with rounds to chamber (else the game's dry click). A release with no magazine now keeps the slide
  back (a visible change: it closed before). The free hand's second-weapon pistol still chambers and closes by itself when a
  loaded magazine goes in (SecondWeapon.CycleForTheHand: one hand cannot rack). OPEN, a decision: guns the analysis gives no
  lock-on-empty (LockOnEmpty needs a slide path or a handgun, WeaponAnalyzer ~4326): the MWII M4s, AS Val family, ARS
  HK416/MP9 bot-friendly, SIGNAL DMR; an AR's bolt does lock on empty. Headset check: the M1911, mwii-g17, ACE 32: fire the
  last round, eject, release (button or trigger), insert a full magazine: the slide stays back; release: it chambers and
  fires with no pull.
- 265 (full auto stops): the plain A press on the gun hand switches the fire mode (2026-10-17, d509c23 / f46b50e; the A is
  bound single = SlideRelease, long = FireMode); GameInputBridge.HandleWeaponButtons did it with the trigger held, so an
  automatic went to semi in the middle of a burst ("press again"). Now no fire-mode change while the trigger (or the support
  hand's fire) is held, and a pending A press is dropped when the trigger is pulled; a sight flip or magnifier still works
  while firing.
- 266 (RPG shake): the RPG-7V [PG-7V] family is a cupped PISTOL in the analysis (rig.json isPistol true): its muzzle
  transform sits in the tube's middle, 0.28 m ahead of the grip, so the cupped-grip rule ran, and IsLauncher (HeldWeapon)
  never matched ('7V V' has no 'rpg'): no steadying, no two-hand steering. Now WeaponRigData.LengthFromGrip also counts the
  drawn barrel's front (PullMuzzleOntoBarrel, single-shot guns whose barrel reaches 0.3 m past the muzzle transform): the
  RPG-7V is 0.9 m long, takes the forend support at 0.24 m, and is a launcher. The plain RPG-7 is a launcher already (its
  muzzle pulled to the barrel's end): its likeliest cause is the world stand-in (HeldWeapon ~1418-1441: the drawn gun blended
  toward where the world holds it, a long tube on the shoulder touching the player's head); not changed. Also unchecked:
  the Javelin and Hydra probably stay cupped pistols.
- 267 (second weapon shows the game's hands): Weapon.Unholster ends with Weapon.Show, which enables every renderer with
  Weapon.arms; HeldWeapon hides them every frame (HideArms), SecondWeapon never did. Now SecondWeapon.BeforeRender hides
  them after Show and PutAway restores them; WeaponDrops leaves w.arms out of its holstered and dropped copies; the autotest's
  second-weapon line says "its own arms hidden / SHOWING (n)".
- 268 (floating magazines): the parked spares (WeaponRigData.SparePartPaths, task 257) were hidden only by the held gun
  (HeldWeapon.HideSpareParts). Now the rig hides and shows them (WeaponRig.HideSpareParts / ShowSpareParts, scales kept at
  construction), used by HeldWeapon, SecondWeapon, and WeaponDrops.HideParkedAmmo on the one template per entry that the
  holster display and every dropped copy are made from (renderers off too, so the dropped collider does not keep them).
- 269 (MWII M4 two handles): the rack point was the grip guess (5 cm behind the grip, 3 cm up: UpperReceiverRear needs
  readable meshes, all 24 are GPU-only), behind the model's own T, and a knob was added there. New ArOwnHandleNoSlide (an
  AR-pattern self-loader with no slide and no handle): the static part 3-12 cm long, at most 2.5 cm tall, at least 2.5 cm
  across, 0-6 cm over the bore, nearest the guess within 10 cm, is the handle: moved by the stroke, rack point at its back
  end; HeldWeapon makes no knob then. The plain and holo M4 have their T as a part; the ACOG's export has none (16 drawn
  parts against 18 and 20), so it may keep the knob: check 'ownhandle:' in the log.
- 270 (pistol magazine through the grip): the magazine's line is its own length (MagAxisFromShape: the principal axis of its
  vertices, 13-21 deg off its box; the 1911's 12.8 deg), which is right for the magazine, but the grip's walls stand near
  upright, and HeldWeapon.FitMagazineLine counted the magazine's BOX against the well, so it saw no walls. Now for a handgun
  the fit counts the magazine's drawn shape in the grip and tilts the line when it goes through (rifles keep the box). Risk:
  a working pistol's line may re-tilt (up to 24 deg, the existing gate). The in-hand keep-out (MagazineProp KeepOffGun,
  ProbeInside) still uses the box. The sandbox shows the analysed line, not the fitted one. Headset / autotest check: the
  M1911, Q1911, FNX-45, GLOCKs: 'magazine line' lines in the log ("by its drawn shape").
- 271 (M4A1 sight racks): fixed by me, see the task line.

### For 262: new workshop mods, found dynamically (2026-10-18 evening)

- Asked by the user while testing in the headset. 5 read-only Haiku scouts (when the analysis runs, caches, names against
  motion and geometry, the 287 exported rigs and the weapons-1017 autotest, the sandbox), each answer with code evidence.
- How it works now (dynamic, no per-weapon list): WeaponWarmup analyses every entry of WeaponManager.allWeapons once the
  game's content has loaded (OnAllContentLoaded) and at VR start; a weapon it has not reached (a secret unlock, one added
  in play) is analysed on first use through WeaponSystem.RigFor (a hitch, never skipped). WeaponAnalyzer samples the
  weapon's own animator: the magazine is the part the reload moves nearest the grip (then a part the reload hides and
  shows, then a name, then a virtual well); the slide is the part the fire clip moves along the bore (then the handle the
  reload pulls straight back, then a name, then a virtual rack point). Names only weight those choices (magazine 3x, slide
  1.5x) or come after motion. WeaponOverrides.json (BepInEx config) corrects one weapon by hand, without code.
- In the data: 287 rigs, magazines 233 from motion, 18 by name, 36 virtual (17 on firearms); racks 213 from motion (169 fire
  clip, 44 reload handle), 13 by name, 61 virtual (40 on firearms). In weapons-1017, 93 of the 101 firearms failing a reload
  check had a motion-found magazine: the failures are mostly seat and rack-point geometry (tasks 252/259/260), not discovery.
- Gaps (to fix, most important first):
  1. The menu's mod reload (after a workshop subscribe, update or mod toggle) rebuilds every WeaponEntry, but
     WeaponAnalyzer.Cache is cleared only when the overrides file changes (WeaponOverrides.cs:78): a same-named updated
     weapon keeps its old rig (old sibling-index paths taken silently) until the game restarts; the warm-up's Warmed set
     and WeaponDrops.Templates skip the new entries too. Clear the analysis, shape, volume and drop caches on content
     reload.
  2. Analyze runs every rule in one try with no catch (WeaponAnalyzer.cs:546-642): one throwing rule replaces the whole rig
     with Fallback (no magazine, slide, sounds, arms) for the session, no retry. WeaponSystem.AttachHeld calls RigFor before
     its try (WeaponSystem.cs:354): a throw there reruns the analysis every frame. Per-rule try/catch, and RigFor inside the try.
  3. The key is entry name + prefab name (WeaponAnalyzer.KeyOf, no source mod): two mods with the same names share one rig,
     one export folder, one pose file; the drop shape key is 'drop:' + entry name only (WeaponDrops.cs:520), the hull key
     'tp:' + entry name (GunVolume): two same-named entries already exist (M4A1#m4 and M4A1#M4A1squad).
  4. Silent fallbacks: a virtual magazine or rack is warned only for SelfLoading and Manual guns (35 firearm rigs have an
     invisible handle with no warning; the P220 akimbo trio a virtual magazine); a missing left hand bone puts the support
     at grip + aim x 0.24 with no warning; rig.json has no handsMethod.
  5. Names that gate whole rules: hand bones need 'hand' + an R/L word (else the grip and support are estimates and the
     hand-driven rack, curls and arm chain go); the palm and grip direction use English finger names only; every unnamed
     mesh directly under the weapon root is left out of the part pool (a flat hierarchy gets a virtual magazine and rack);
     open-bolt rest, AR / Bren 2 / Garand / Vector families, the cover by name (or auto with 50+ rounds).
  6. Smaller: a sub-weapon (under-barrel launcher) is analysed on the prefab before its Start, so its animator is not
     sampled (5 rigs all virtual); absolute travel limits (pistol slide 7 cm, rifle bolt 23 cm); round words (Round, Shell,
     子弹) weighted as magazine words; overrides matched by substring (one meant for one gun hits any name containing it);
     the warm-up coroutine has no finally (one escaping throw stops the warm-up for the session); mounted sentries analysed
     needlessly.
  7. The sandbox lists only exported weapons: a new mod's weapons appear after an export (Advanced, Export weapons) and
     nothing says so; 19 analysed keys (13 sub-weapons, 6 zombie hands) are never exported; folders of removed mods stay.
- One scout wrote two scratch files into %TEMP% despite a read-only brief (key lists); removed.

### For 260: the user's magazine items (2026-10-18)

- (a) Mag kick: the knocked-out magazine flies forward along the barrel and tumbles end over end, top leading (it fell
  sideways and down spinning about the barrel). Fixer agent (ReloadInteractions.KickOut); in the game (check-1018f) the
  HK416's mag kick still knocks out on a fast strike, a slow push and tilted 45 degrees, and nothing passing beside it.
- (c) A seated magazine is solid for a magazine in the hand: the well's opening no longer lets it through
  (MagazineProp.KeepOffGun, HeldWeapon.SeatedMagSolid); the mag kick still triggers (above).
- (b) "the hitbox is down and not on the whole mag": NOT reproduced yet. The fixer changed ShapeFit.Thin (farthest points
  per direction instead of every n-th): no change in the autotest (FNX-45 53% covered before and after, HK416 52%). Then
  measured: the HK416's and FNX-45's physics pieces span exactly the drawn copy (log 'Magazine shape of ...', check-1018g),
  the grab distance is measured to those colliders, and the keep-out's 25 points run from the drawn base to the drawn tip;
  no partial-hull warnings in the Unity log; over all 244 guns with a magazine the analysis box sits within 1 cm of the
  drawn magazine except 8 (the ARS Bot Friendly HK416 / MP9 / G19 at 6-10 cm, a G BRN180 EO SS at 17 (its drum), M500,
  Panzerfaust 3). The autotest's ~52% coverage is measured on a thrown (flying, curved) magazine against its rendered
  surface at 5 mm, not proof of a short shape. Needed from the user: which guns, and which hitbox (picking a magazine up,
  a magazine in the hand bumping the gun, a dropped magazine lying on the ground).

### For 259: rack points and grips from the scouts' list (2026-10-18)

- Rack stroke from the reload hand (RackStrokeFromHands): on all 7 guns that took it (AS Val x2, AK-24 x2, ACE 32,
  AK-105 x2) the reload hand's palm centre waited 5 to 8 cm from every part, and the rack point (and a handle cut out
  there) hung in the air. Now a handle is cut out only where some part of the gun is within 2.5 cm (the arms' meshes
  left out); else the stroke is not used and the rack point stays on the moving part.
- RackOnOutstandingPiece: a carrier made in one mesh with its handle (no small piece to find) is taken by its vertex
  farthest from the bore, on the receiver's stretch, 8 mm in from that tip; not for a pump's forend (its centre 31 to
  35 cm ahead of the grip; the rifles' are at most 23). 23 guns: AS Val (on its hook), QBZ192 / QBU191 family (14, on the
  paddle), ACE 32 (top left, under its sight), CZ Bren 2 BR family (their own side handle still wins after). Meshes that
  cannot be read (AK-24, AK-105) keep the rack point on the carrier.
- In the game (check-1018c): AS Val 4/4 fingers on the moving part (before: the hand in the air), QBZ192-A 1/4 (the
  index on the paddle), ACE 32 0/4 (the fingers on the top of the receiver under the red dot: its handle is on the left,
  bent up under the sight), AK-24 2/4, AK-105 0/4 (on the carrier, deep in the receiver). Open: ACE 32 and AK-105.
- AR charging handle (ArChargingHandle): "5 cm behind the grip point" lay on the buffer tube behind the receiver of the
  M417A2 and Suzuya's AR-15s (their grip point low on a slanted grip; the hand closed on air in the game, 0/4 fingers).
  Now the upper receiver's back end is measured (walking back along the top, 2.5 to 7 cm above the bore, until a gap over
  1.5 cm) and the handle is taken just ahead of it: the sandbox shows the point on both T-handles. Guns whose meshes cannot
  be read (HK416 BotFriendly, the mwii M4s) keep the estimate.
- Panzerfaust 3: its grips (铁拳3 握把, 前握把) moved in the reload and had its rocket's size, so the spare rule hid them
  and both hands held air. The spare rule now never takes a grip, handle, key or release by name (the parked-ammunition
  rule's list). Its hands still hung 6 to 8 cm under the grips (the animation's palms): for launchers only, a grip named
  as such (握把, pistol grip; 前握把, foregrip) more than 4 cm from the palm takes the grip / support point (its back face,
  1.2 cm off). Tried for every gun first: the MK46's right hand went onto its vertical foregrip (named "GRIP") and the
  MK18 RIS III's onto its Grip-N-Rip charging handle, so it was narrowed to launchers.
- In the game (check-1018d): the M417A2's and the Suzuya AR-15's hands now close on the T-handle at the upper receiver's back (before: on the buffer tube); the test's 0/4 counts the bolt inside, not the handle cut out. The Panzerfaust's grip rule first ran right after the hands: its rocket became the grip (握把) and it did not fire; moved to the end of the analysis, the rocket is again what check-1018c fired with (铁拳3 枪托; whether that is the right part is a question of its own: its warhead is 铁拳3_炮弹). NOT yet checked in the game after that move: run the Panzerfaust 3 again. The CZ Bren 2's side knob is now taken at a vertex of the knob (the band's point nearest its mean), not checked in the game either.
- check-1018e (2026-10-18 ~15:50): the Panzerfaust 3 loads and fires again (1/1, the rocket loaded by hand), its grips drawn and held. That run crawled at 113 ms a frame (median 18, stalls up to 7.8 s): not the mod (its sections under 1 ms a frame, the GPU 5 ms) but the PC out of memory, 60 of 74 GB committed by ~45 Python backtest workers of another Claude session (Desktop/Trader); the next start failed with 'the paging file is too small'. Its shake readings are not trustworthy: the CZ Bren 2 BR alone (lag-bren, 16 ms a frame) held still at 0.07 mm. The CZ Bren 2 BR Raid's 'slide stayed back 93 mm' was its 'Red Dot Sight', a part the sight mode moves: the check's DrawnLayout now leaves sight-mode parts out (as laser dots). To re-run clean: the Panzerfaust's two-hand steadiness.
- check-1018h (2026-10-18 17:31, the PC free: 17 of 55 GB committed; Panzerfaust 3 + CZ Bren 2 BR Raid, VR null driver): frames avg 9.8 ms (median 9.1, 99th 18.3). Panzerfaust 3 held still with two hands: 0.00 deg / 0.0 mm a frame (check-1018e's 1.95 deg / 12.9 mm was the starved PC), loaded by hand and fired; the second weapon in the free hand now fires (8 shots; NOT FIRED in 1018e). CZ Bren 2 BR Raid: the rack's moving part 1.5 cm from the hand (was 3.3 cm, the 'part under the hand does not move' flag gone). Still flagged: its magazine half way in shows 33% beside the gun's sides (GOES IN BESIDE THE WELL; seated 13%), the open magazine-deep side test.
- AK-105 / AK-24: their carrier meshes cannot be read at runtime (only the sandbox export reads them back from the GPU), and the box alone puts the handle 1 cm off on the AK-24 but 9 cm off on the AK-105 (its carrier runs past the tab): left until the mod reads such meshes back itself.
- Estimated grips without a hand bone (QBZ192-D's QLG, the M320, the AR-15 launcher, the ARS Bot Friendly guns): harmless for the launchers in the game (the gun is held by the parent rifle's rig; the sub weapon's rig is used for its reload and recoil only).

### For 258: the sandbox's transform gizmos (2026-10-18)

- gizmo.py (numpy only, no GL): Camera (projection, rays, metres per pixel), build() (screen-space triangles: arrows with
  heads, plane squares, the centre circle, the axis rings' front halves, the white view ring, the trackball sphere; while
  dragging only the handle taken, with the axis line or the angle's pie), hit() (the centre first, then the nearest line
  within 7 px; the trackball last), Drag (axis: the ray's nearest point on the axis line; plane: the ray on the plane;
  ring: the angle in the ring's own plane, so a drag along a tilted ring turns exactly as far as the ring shows; nearly
  edge-on rings turn by the drag along their near side; Shift a tenth, Ctrl 1 cm / 5 degrees, 1 mm / 1 degree with Shift;
  a move never more than 1.5 m).
- viewport.py: the handles come before the hand's body (whose free drag stays), the trackball only where no hand is under
  the pointer; Ctrl + click on the gun puts the gizmo on the gun (at its grip, the gun faintly lit); the moves are applied
  from the drag's start (no drift); Esc or a right click takes a drag back and drops its undo step.
- main.py: the header over the view (Free / Move / Turn / Move + turn, Axes, Snap, On Hand / Gun), keys W G R T and comma,
  the choice remembered (not by the self-test); the readout at the top left while dragging; clean pictures leave the gizmo
  out. Help, the hints and tools/sandbox/README.md say how.
- Checked: 184 headless math checks (5 views incl. orthographic and edge-on rings, global and turned axes); self-test
  86/86 on HK416-A, 82/82 on GLOCK 43X, with 29 gizmo checks driven by real mouse events, measured against the whole
  pixels the view gets (arrow along X only, Ctrl snap 2.7 -> 3 cm, the YZ square in its plane, the Z ring 30 degrees
  about Z with the glove kept in place, snapped 31.7 -> 30, Esc and right click take the drag back with the hand's part
  and job and no undo step and no menu, a click alone leaves no undo step and keeps the redo history, the wheel during a
  drag does not zoom, local axes, the gizmo on the gun moves it with the hands and the laser pick, keys, Free keeps the
  free drag, a clean picture draws no gizmo).
- Review (2026-10-18, 4 Haiku finders: math, view, app, Blender; a Haiku skeptic per finder; a completeness critic). Fixed:
  a right click that cancels opened the context menu; on a settled hand the gizmo sat on the glove but the drag moved the
  place (a 2 cm jump, the axis line off): a gizmo drag now takes the drawn place first, so neither jumps (the critic
  preferred the gizmo on the glove over the place); a cancel left the hand's part and job cleared (now the drag's undo
  step is restored); a click on a handle, a hand or Ctrl + click on the gun left an undo step that changed nothing and
  cleared the snap, and wiped the redo history; keys (1, 2, gizmo keys, Ctrl+Z, O, F), the wheel and a middle press during
  a drag changed what it measured from; the trackball took presses on the gun inside the rings (now only where nothing is
  under the pointer, lit only there) and ignored Ctrl (now it turns from where it began and snaps); a Ctrl-snapped move in
  the view's plane left the plane; an arrow dragged past its vanishing point threw the hand behind the eye, a plane near
  its horizon spiked to 1.5 m; edge-on rings turned 5 % too far; arrows drawn faded could not be taken; the hover light
  stayed after a drag; the gun's Local axes were named X, Y, Z; a plane square was named by its plane, not its colour;
  rings were built in Python loops (now numpy, and hit tests stop far from the gizmo); clean pictures kept the corner axis
  key; a drag during a reload fought the timeline (now the reload is run to its end first); a laser pick stayed where the
  gun had been; the self-test could read the user's saved repository setting; three self-test checks could pass while
  the feature was broken.
- Not done, with reasons: W / G / R / T kept (W for no gizmo matches Blender's default keymap, where W is the select tool;
  G and R its move and rotate); a modal G / R grab with X / Y / Z constraint keys (Blender's operators) is a possible next
  step, not asked for (the user asked for the arrows and rings).

### For 255: 30 read-only Haiku agents (20 hunters for performance, bugs and improvements, 10 skeptics) on 2026-10-17 morning

111 findings: 91 confirmed by the skeptics (47 bugs, 31 performance, 13 improvements), 20 refuted. Ids: P = performance, B = bugs, I = improvements (P1/B1 HeldWeapon, P2/B2 ReloadInteractions, P3/B3 MagazineProp and props, P4/I2 hands, P5/B5 rig, UI and input, P6/I4 volumes and analysis, P7/I5 systems and launchers/grenades, B4/B6 analysis and the reload state machine, B7/I1 pose and magazines, I3/I6 machine guns and tests).

- [x] 255.B1-1 (bug, major, skeptic high) src/Weapons/HeldWeapon.cs:898: Loaded-rest pose is lost per holding; drawn-empty guns never get the fix. _loadedRestState and _emptyState are instance fields (line 849). A new HeldWeapon is built on every draw (WeaponSystem.cs:314) and dropped on holster (WeaponSystem.cs:332-337), so the learned loaded pose resets to 0. The check at line 898 requires _loadedRestState != 0, so a gun drawn while empty never sends its animator back to the loaded pose. Worse, _lastShotAt starts at ... Trigger: Empty a pistol of the FNX-45 family (animator sits in its empty pose), holster it, draw it again, insert a magazine and rack or release the slide. Within 0.3 s now == _emptyState, but ... Fix: Keep the learned loaded and empty states per weapon type in a static dictionary keyed by WeaponRigData, as MagLineFitted does, and read the stored loaded state when the instance value is 0. Guard the record at lines 917-919 with 'now != _emptyState' so an empty pose cannot be stored as the loaded rest. (The guard belongs at 917-919, not at line 905.)
- [x] 255.B2-1 (bug, major, skeptic high) src/Weapons/ReloadInteractions.cs:65: Support-trigger launcher switch-back is dropped while the launcher reloads. On release, SupportTrigger switches back to the saved rifle slot only if the launcher is not reloading (line 65), then clears _launcherFrom (line 69) either way. Nothing retries. The Update() revert (lines 307-316) has the same guard and the same clear. The game never switches sub weapons by itself: EquipSubWeapon is called only from this file and from the player's fire-mode ... Trigger: Fire the under-barrel launcher's last round with the game's auto-reload on (Weapon.cs ~1382 Invoke AutoReload -> Reload sets reloading=true), and let go of the support trigger while that reload is ... Fix: Keep _launcherFrom pending while the launcher is reloading. Retry EquipSubWeapon(_launcherFrom.Value) each frame in Update, before the !off.Tracked return (line 293), when launcher is active and not reloading. Clear _launcherFrom only after the switch. The SupportTrigger branch at lines 55-60 already handles a re-press without overwriting it.
- [x] 255.I2-1 (bug, major, skeptic high) src/Rig/HandModel.cs:469: Keep-out search checks last frame's glove position, a fast hand enters the gun. ClearanceAlong (line 469) and ShortestWayOut (line 497) start from tracked = -_graspOffset - _keepOut, which assumes the hand is where it was at the last Apply. The skin they test (_skinA/_skinB and PalmSkin, posed in Apply and stored by UpdateSkin at lines 810-828) is from the last frame, but the tracked Palm was refreshed this frame in VRRig.BeforeRender (VRRig.cs:767) ... Trigger: A free hand or magazine hand swung at about 3 m/s moves about 3.3 cm in one 11 ms frame at 90 fps. The search says the glove is clear at a shift that is short by that much, so the glove sits that far ... Fix: In UpdateSkin, also store _skinRoot = _root.position (after Apply's offsets). In ClearanceAlong and ShortestWayOut, use tracked = PlacedTarget().position + _graspOffset - _skinRoot. Rotation stays one frame behind (a few mm on a fast turn).
- [x] 255.I2-2 (bug, major, skeptic medium) src/Rig/HandModel.cs:647: Keep-out growth is immediate, so the glove can jump 12 cm in one frame. Line 647 applies any growth of _keepOut at once (wantOut.sqrMagnitude >= _keepOut.sqrMagnitude). Two cases jump. ClearanceAlong returns the full max when no step is clear (line 481: return already > 0.001f ? already : max), which for magazines is 0.12 m. And the test compares only lengths, so a direction flip at equal length (ShortestWayOut picking up instead of down between ... Trigger: A magazine lined up under the well with the glove sideways on the well floor, nothing clear within 12 cm: the glove teleports 12 cm back in a single frame (KeepOutOfGun, ... Fix: Rate-limit growth as the hunter proposed: _keepOut = Vector3.MoveTowards(_keepOut, wantOut, 4f * dt), which takes about 3 frames for 12 cm. Verify with MagazineKeepOutCheck (ReloadInteractions.cs:3348) and the 7 cm penetration case before keeping it. A narrower option is to keep immediate growth in length but ease when the direction changes.
- [x] 255.I2-3 (bug, major, skeptic medium) src/Weapons/MagazineProp.cs:1924: Two keep-outs written to one hand per frame, the shorter one is dropped. ReloadInteractions.BeforeRender runs UpdateGraspAnchor (line 2051), which for a magazine lined up under the well but not yet on the rail calls KeepOutOfGun (line 2153) and sets off.Model.KeepOut = -insert * back (line 2253). Then FollowHand (line 2089) calls KeepOffGun, and line 1924 keeps only the longer of the two: model.KeepOut = setThisFrame && model.KeepOut.sqrMagnitude > ... Trigger: Magazine lined up about 2 cm sideways out of the well into a pistol grip wall, with a 3 cm clearance along the insert. The glove moves back 3 cm but not the 2 cm sideways, so the palm can stay up to ... Fix: At MagazineProp.cs:1946 use model.KeepOut = setThisFrame ? model.KeepOut + _keptOut : _keptOut. Both shifts move the glove out of the gun (the gun shift backs it out of the well, _keptOut moves it toward the magazine's drawn point outside the gun), so the sum errs toward being out.
